# WORKSTATUS

Single source of truth for work history, current status, validation, unresolved issues and next steps (see `AGENTS.md`).
The previous log was removed in commit `b741337`; this file restarts it.

## Current Status

- **Create Sales Order — Incoterms-missing-in-VA03 diagnosed; resolution is SAP master-data defaulting, no repo change (2026-10-08):** Re-confirmed across UI, CAP `OrderHeader` contract, mapper, and adapter that Incoterms is never captured/mapped/posted, and that the create service `LORD_ODATA_ORDER_SRV` `Header` entity has **zero** Incoterms properties (`grep -ic incoterm` on its `.edmx` = 0). User chose the master-data path: maintain INCO1/INCO2 on the sold-to customer master for the order's sales area so SAP defaults VBKD at creation; verify on a **new** order in VA03/VBKD. No code changed, no SAP data modified. Explicit per-order entry would require re-pointing create to `API_SALES_ORDER_SRV` (large, verification-gated — deferred). See Changes Log 2026-10-08 and Unresolved Issue 10(a).
- **Movement Type 261 attempt log — a retry after a provably-not-posted attempt now re-claims on its own (2026-10-08):** `GoodsIssueAttemptStore.createOrGet` takes an opt-in re-claim policy; the 261 post handler re-claims on `rejected`/`not_posted` and the reverse handler on `rejected` **only** (never `not_posted`, because `CancelItem` stamps the 262 with the original 261's reference so a posted reversal can be mislabelled). Old rows are kept (generation-suffixed reference = audit trail); concurrent re-claims → exactly one wins; live/done attempts still 409. The `unconfirmed`→`not_posted` recheck timer runs in any non-`test` runtime (201-outcome confirmation only; 261/262-aware confirmation deferred — trust MB51). QA_261.md gained rows #24/#25, a re-claim section, and a stuck-attempt runbook (MB51 first; DBA-only row deletion on an approved ticket). Tests: `mvt261Scan.test.js` 41/41 (+6). **No live SAP data was modified.**
- **Movement Type 261 Scan screen — supply-area stock shown honestly, not suggested as consumable for a non-WM reservation (2026-10-08):** For reservation 518006/3 (material 1000000653, non-WM issue location PT01, supply area `PRVBE = IP05 → PVBE.LGORT = CS01`), the 25 KG (batch IN25072189) sits in the supply area CS01 while PT01 is empty. After the earlier "production-related stock" change the scan list showed that CS01 unit as `Available`/`Suggested`, so scanning it led to the post-time `unrestricted stock 0 KG is less than 25 KG` (the 261 posts from PT01). Fix (app-side, read/display only — no write-path change): `Mvt261Adapter.scanContext` now suggests a unit only when it is issuable by this reservation — `c.Warehouse ? true : q.LGORT === reservation LGORT` — so for a non-WM reservation supply-area stock in another location is shown flagged `OnHold`/`inSupplyArea` (not suggested); it also returns `SupplyAreaStock` (qty stranded in the PSA location), and the view shows a warning strip ("N KG in supply area CS01 — this reservation issues from PT01; transfer required"). CDS `ScanContext` gains `SupplyAreaStock`; i18n `scan261Reject_inSupplyArea` + `scan261SupplyAreaStock` added to both bundles; controller gains a `formatMessage` helper. **`checkStorageUnit` deliberately unchanged** — a manual scan of a different storage location keeps the existing warn-but-accept behavior (T7), and the post-time check still correctly refuses. **Underlying stock discrepancy is SAP master data, not code** (see investigation 2026-10-08 below): the reservation carries supply area IP05 (WM-PP/W13 control cycle `PKHD 0007233`) but a non-WM location PT01, and the last 25 KG is stranded in CS01 — fix in SAP by transferring CS01→PT01 (311) or correcting the component's storage-location determination. Validation: `npx jest test/unit/wm` 52 suites / 1071 tests (new scanContext non-WM case), `npx cds compile`, `ui5lint` (0 findings), `git diff --check`, and live read-only `scanContext(518006,3)` (CS01 unit → OnHold/inSupplyArea/not-suggested, `SupplyAreaStock` 25) — all pass.
- **Movement Type 261 Scan screen — warehouse mismatch no longer piles on a misleading staging shortfall (2026-10-08):** When the reservation's warehouse (`RESB-LGNUM`, e.g. W13) differs from the storage location's configured warehouse (`T320`, e.g. W01), the scan block message was `transmitted warehouse number is W13; determined warehouse number is W01; available stock shortfall of 370 KG in interim storage bin IP5/0001002619` — two reasons, the second derivative: the staging check ran in the *determined* warehouse (W01), not the transmitted one (W13), so it reported a full shortfall that is just a symptom of the mismatch. Fix: `Mvt261Adapter.cycle` computes `warehouseMismatch` (same condition as `blockers()`) and gates `stagingShortfall` on `!warehouseMismatch`, so a mismatched item shows only the actionable warehouse-mismatch reason (and `StagingShortfall` is 0) until the warehouse is aligned. Live read-only confirmed on order 1002619 (reservation 479284 items 3 & 4): block reason is now `transmitted warehouse number is W13; determined warehouse number is W01` alone. Validation: `npx jest test/unit/wm` 52 suites / 1070 tests (new cycle case), `git diff --check` — pass.
- **Movement Type 261 Open Items Worklist (`#/wm/mvt261/open`) — warehouse shown in a "Plant / SLoc / WH" column (2026-10-08):** The existing Plant / SLoc column now also shows the configured warehouse. `Mvt261Adapter.openItems` resolves the warehouse per item from `T320` (new `_warehouses(items)` helper, one read per distinct plant, keyed `plant|storageLocation`) and sets `Warehouse` on each item (`''` when the storage location is not warehouse-managed); CDS `Open261Item` gains `Warehouse : String(3)`; `Open261.view.xml` column header is `open261PlantSlocWh` ("Plant / SLoc / WH") with cell `{Plant} / {StorageLocation} / {Warehouse || '-'}`; i18n key `open261PlantSlocWh` added to both bundles. Live read-only confirmed: reservation 519944 items in plant 1130 / CS02 → `W12`, a ST03 item → `-` (not WM-managed). Validation: `npx jest test/unit/wm` 52 suites / 1068 tests (new openItems warehouse case), `npx cds compile`, `ui5lint` (0 findings), `git diff --check` — all pass.
- **Movement Type 261 Scan screen (`#/wm/mvt261/open/{reservation}/{item}`) — stock list restricted to production-related stock for the order (2026-10-08):** The per-item scan/PGI screen now shows only stock relevant to the order component: (a) the reservation item's material (as before), (b) in the reservation's storage location **and** the order's production supply area location, (c) the reserved batch only when the item is batch-specific, (d) within the order's warehouse. Goods issue is allowed for **released (REL) orders only**: a non-released order shows an info message and an empty list. Non-unrestricted stock (QI/blocked/returns/in-TO) is still shown but flagged (user decision). PSA source proven live read-only: `RESB.PRVBE → PVBE(WERKS,PRVBE).LGORT` (reservation 519944/6 → PRVBE `IP02-2F-HU` → CS02). Changes: `Mvt261Adapter.cycle` reads `RESB.PRVBE` + one `PVBE` row and exposes `SupplyArea`/`SupplyAreaStorageLocation`; `scanContext` filters LQUA by {reservation LGORT ∪ PSA LGORT} ∩ warehouse ∩ reserved batch and empties the list for non-REL, exposing `Batch`/`SupplyArea`/`SupplyAreaStorageLocation`/`OrderReleased`; `checkStorageUnit` rejects a scan whose batch ≠ the reserved batch (`wrongBatch`); CDS `Cycle261`/`ScanContext` extended; `Scan261.view.xml` adds a production-scope info strip, a non-released info strip, open qty in the units header, and empty-state "No production stock for this order"; i18n keys added to both bundles. **Known gap (user decision):** mvt261 enforces no `plantScope` (unlike handling-unit); verify-test 5 (plant A/B isolation) is therefore N/A. **Not executed:** live PGI post (needs the user's explicit OK). Validation: `npx jest test/unit/wm` 52 suites / 1068 tests (new scanContext batch/PSA/release + checkStorageUnit wrongBatch cases), `npx cds compile`, `ui5lint` (0 findings), `git diff --check`, and live read-only `scanContext(519944,6)` — all pass; stock in CS01 correctly excluded, PSA stock in CS02 included.
- **Movement Type 261 Open Items Worklist — interim-staging shortfall now classified Blocked in the list, consistent with the scan page (2026-10-08):** Fixed the inconsistency where a WM-PP staged item short in its interim bin (e.g. `Scanning is not possible: available stock shortfall of 712 KG in interim storage bin IP1/0001002819`) appeared in the **Open** tab of the list but was blocked when opened on the scan page. Root cause: `Mvt261Adapter.openItems()` readiness/block logic checked order status, movement-allowed, release and ready storage units, but never the interim-staging shortfall that `cycle()`/scan apply, so such items were marked `ScanPossible` in the list. Fix: new `Mvt261Adapter._stagingShortfalls(items)` reproduces the exact `cycle()` check via two batch reads per plant (RESB for `LGTYP`/bin of staged items, LQUA for staged stock in `LGTYP`/bin), and `openItems()` now sets the same block reason (`available stock shortfall of <qty> <unit> in interim storage bin <type>/<bin>`) — placed before the "No ready storage units" heuristic so the list and scan page agree. No CDS/UI change: `Open261Item.Blocked`/`BlockReason`/`ScanPossible` and the IconTabBar already consume these. Validation: `npx jest test/unit/wm` 52 suites / 1064 tests (2 new openItems cases: shortfall → Blocked with the scan reason, sufficient stock → scannable), `git diff --check` — pass.
- **Movement Type 261 Goods Issue — WM-managed location returns outbound delivery, not a false 502 (2026-10-08):** Fixed the `postGoodsIssue` 502 Bad Gateway (`SAP did not return a material document for the goods issue. sap-message: L9/514 "Delivery 80000087 created"`). For a WM-managed storage location SAP answers the `API_MATERIAL_DOCUMENT_SRV` 261 POST with HTTP 201, an empty `MaterialDocument`, and `sap-message L9/514` because it converts the goods movement into an outbound delivery; the goods issue completes only when PGI is posted for that delivery. (1) `Mvt261Adapter.postGoodsIssue` now reuses `GoodsIssuePostingClient.parseSapMessage` + `deliveryFromSapMessage` (the exact path the 201 flow already uses) — on an empty material document with an L9/514 delivery it returns `{ MaterialDocument:'', DeliveryNumber, Pending:true, Message }` instead of throwing; a truly empty response with no delivery still throws 502. `_post` now returns the response headers. (2) `service.cds` `MaterialDocumentResult` gains `DeliveryNumber`, `Pending`, `Message`. (3) `service.js` records the delivery outcome as attempt status `not_posted` carrying `DeliveryNumber` (not `posted`, because no GI posted yet). (4) `Scan261.controller.js` shows a warning with the delivery number and the "PGI required" note (new i18n key `scan261PostDelivery`) instead of a misleading "material document posted" success. **Not done — needs the user's go (live SAP write, UNVERIFIED per Unresolved Issues):** auto-posting PGI on the delivery via the existing `OutboundDeliveryAdapter.postGoodsIssue`. Validation: `npx jest test/unit/wm` 52 suites / 1062 tests, `npx cds compile srv/wm/mvt261/service.cds`, `ui5lint` on the changed controller (0 findings), `git diff --check` — all pass.
- **Movement Type 261 Open Items Worklist (`#/wm/mvt261/open`) — IconTabBar Segmentation for Open, Blocked, and All Items (2026-10-08):** Implemented Fiori `IconTabBar` to segment Movement Type 261 items into **Open** (128 items ready for scanning/issue), **Blocked** (292 items blocked by unreleased/locked order, movement disallowed, or no stock), and **All** (420 items). (1) `Mvt261Adapter.openItems()` now calculates and returns `Blocked: Boolean` and `BlockReason: String` (naming exact cause: "Order not released", "Order locked/closed/deleted", "Movement not allowed", "No ready storage units in stock", or "No open quantity remaining") alongside `ScanPossible`. (3) `Open261.view.xml` replaces redundant switch with `<IconTabBar>` containing filters for `open` (`sap-icon://sys-enter-2`, count: 128), `blocked` (`sap-icon://error`, count: 292), and `all` (`sap-icon://list`, count: 420); table displays status icon/text and block reason. (4) `Open261.controller.js` manages instant client-side tab switching (`_applyTabFilter()`), maintains tab counts, preserves DESC sorting, and exports status text and block reason. (5) `test/unit/wm/open261Controller.test.js` verified with 5 tests covering initialization, onGo tab counts, tab selection switching, sort toggling, and cycle navigation. All 52 WM suites (1,061/1,061 tests), `ui5lint`, and `git diff --check` pass.
- **Movement Type 261 Open Items Worklist (`#/wm/mvt261/open`) — Default DESC Sort & Dynamic Sort Toggle (2026-10-08):** Set default sort order for Open Movement Type 261 items (128 items under default scan-ready filter) to DESC by requirement date, reservation number, and reservation item. (1) `Mvt261Adapter.openItems()` sends `$orderby: 'MatlCompRequirementDate desc,Reservation desc,ReservationItem desc'` to SAP S/4HANA OData service `UI_RESERVATION_ITM_MNG_V2`, fetching newest reservations (e.g. `523651`, `523650`) first. (2) `Open261.controller.js` implements stable sorting helper `sortItems(aItems, bDesc)` on `RequirementDate`, `Reservation`, and `ReservationItem`, initializes model with `sortDescending: true`, applies DESC sorting on query response, and provides `onToggleSort()` to switch between DESC and ASC. (3) `Open261.view.xml` adds `open261Sort` button in the table header toolbar displaying dynamic icon (`sap-icon://sort-descending` / `sap-icon://sort-ascending`) and text (`DESC` / `ASC`). (4) Added i18n keys for sort labels and tooltips in both `i18n.properties` and `i18n_en.properties`. (5) Added unit tests in `mvt261Adapter.test.js` and dedicated `test/unit/wm/open261Controller.test.js` (4 tests). All 52 WM suites (1,060/1,060 tests), `ui5lint`, and `git diff --check` pass.
- **Movement Type 261 Goods Issue — Strict Storage Location Warehouse Enforcement across Cycle, ScanContext, and CheckStorageUnit (2026-10-08):** Enforced that the warehouse determined for the Storage Location from `T320` (`WERKS` + `LGORT` -> `LGNUM`) is the authoritative warehouse boundary across all Movement Type 261 WM operations: (1) In `cycle()`, filtered `LQUA` quants by `(!configuredWarehouse || q.LGNUM === configuredWarehouse)` so stray/historical quants from disparate warehouses (e.g. `W13` in `CS01`) are excluded from cycle stock and staging checks. (2) In `scanContext()`, filtered FIFO storage units and quants by `(!c.Warehouse || q.LGNUM === c.Warehouse)` in addition to `c.StorageLocation`; exposed `Warehouse` in `ScanContext` (service.cds and payload). (3) In `checkStorageUnit()`, validated scanned storage unit quants against `configuredWarehouse`, rejecting scans with `wrongWarehouse` (Value1: quant warehouse, Value2: storage location warehouse) if scanned SU quants belong to a different warehouse. (4) In `Scan261.view.xml`, displayed `scan261Warehouse` in the ObjectHeader statuses. (5) In i18n, added `scan261Reject_wrongWarehouse` message. (6) All 128 test suites (2,245/2,245 tests repo-wide), 51 WM test suites (1,055/1,055 tests), `ui5lint`, `eslint`, and `git diff --check` pass.
- **Movement Type 261 Goods Issue — Fast-Fail Pre-Validation for Warehouse Mismatch & Interim Staging Shortfall (2026-10-07):** Added pre-validation guards to prevent recurrence of SAP HTTP 400 errors (`Transmitted warehouse number is ...; determined warehouse number is ...` and `Available stock shortfall of ... in interim storage bin`). (1) `Mvt261Adapter.js` reads `LGNUM`, `LGTYP`, `LGPLA` from `RESB` and checks against `T320` configuration: if `RESB-LGNUM` is set and differs from `T320-LGNUM`, `Reservation` and `GoodsIssue` steps are blocked with reason `transmitted warehouse number is <resb>; determined warehouse number is <t320>`. `checkStorageUnit` rejects immediately with `itemBlocked` and this message (0 LQUA lookups). (2) For items with WM-PP staging (`RESB-LGTYP` populated), reads `LQUA` quants in the interim staging bin (`LGTYP/LGPLA` or `LGTYP/AUFNR`). If staged available stock < open/issue quantity, `WmStaging` and `GoodsIssue` steps are blocked with reason `available stock shortfall of <qty> in interim storage bin <type>/<bin>`. (3) In `postGoodsIssue`, explicitly validates warehouse alignment and interim staged stock before making any SAP POST call, throwing HTTP 422 if mismatched or short, guaranteeing zero invalid SAP POST calls. (4) CDS `Cycle261` model enriched with `ReservationWarehouse`, `StagingRequired`, `StagingStorageType`, `StagingBin`, `StagedQuantity`, `StagingShortfall`. All 51 WM test suites (1051/1051 tests), `ui5lint`, `eslint`, and `git diff --check` pass.

- **Handling Unit Label Printing — 4×4 label re-laid-out (2026-10-07):** Per pasted spec, `HuLabelPrinter.js` renders top→bottom: **MATERIAL NAME** (2-line clamp) → **Code 128 barcode** (full width, 1.4in) with the **HU number** (10pt bold) directly below it → **CREATED DATE** (left) + **SR NO** (right) row (pushed to the bottom via `.code{flex:1}`). Both callers already pass `{header, items, serials}`, so **no controller change needed**. - **Handling Unit Label Printing — selectable stock size (2026-10-07, supersedes the fixed 4×4):** Every Print/Download action (6 list/row + detail `onPrint`) now opens a **Select label size** dialog offering **A 50×25 / B 100×50 / C 150×100 / D 200×100 mm** (B default, remembered per browser); the chosen size drives **one shared geometry spec** (`util/HuLabelLayout.js`) used by both the HTML print builder and the jsPDF renderer so the two match, with a per-size barcode-scannability gate that routes too-dense HUs to a larger size. Cancel = no-op. jsPDF stays lazy; no new deps. Full `npx jest test/unit` **128 suites / 2212 tests**, `ui5lint` clean, uiConsistency 7/7, `git diff --check` clean; PDF page sizes + barcode decode verified via Chrome/`pdfinfo`/`zxing`; the size dialog verified live in a backend-less `ui5 serve`. **Pending (needs the user's SAP login): the end-to-end print/download with live `labels()` data, and the physical thermal-stock + scanner checks in the manual checklist.**

Nothing was posted in either attempt. **Conclusion: the Goods Receipt posting path `MMIM_GR4PO_DL_SRV/GR4PO_DL_Headers` has never produced a material document and is not proven (Unresolved Issue 8).** The user chose to prove `API_MATERIAL_DOCUMENT_SRV` (12:20 entry): metadata read, one 100 KG 101 POST on PO 400000164/10 + delivery 180000008/10 with batch IN25000333 → **HTTP 400 VLA 317 "Inbound delivery batch cannot be changed to IN25000333 here"**, no document. 11. **`API_SALES_ORDER_SRV` (the standard transactional Sales Order create API that exposes Incoterms) is not routable on this system — no System Alias.** Live read-only probe 2026-10-08: `GET /sap/opu/odata/sap/API_SALES_ORDER_SRV/$metadata` and the service root both return **HTTP 500 `No System Alias found for Service 'ZAPI_SALES_ORDER_SRV_0001' and user 'KHUSHAL'`**; `catalog-audit.csv` records it (and `ZCREATE_SO_SRV`) as 500. Auth + destination work (the Gateway returned a structured error that names the user), but the service is catalogued without a backend alias — the **same class of blocker as Issue 9** (HU services, fixed by assigning alias `LOCAL`). So its `$metadata` cannot be read and the Incoterms `creatable` flags are **unverified**; no POST was attempted (per the user: do not write to SAP). Fix = Basis assigns System Alias `LOCAL` in `/IWFND/MAINT_SERVICE`, then re-run AGENTS.md discovery (`$metadata` → confirm `IncotermsClassification`/`IncotermsTransferLocation`/`IncotermsLocation1/2` creatable → one minimal POST read back in VA03/VBKD) before any app wiring. Blocks the explicit per-order Incoterms path (option B of Unresolved Issue 10 / Changes Log 2026-10-08). **No SAP data written.**
   - **Landscape scan 2026-10-08 (read-only, all 1,243 status-200 services — regenerate via `tools/scan-incoterms.py` → `incoterms-capability-scan.csv`, gitignored like the other analysis CSVs):** 99 services expose Incoterms properties, but **none can create or directly update Incoterms on a sales order**. All 99 are analytics CDS queries (`cre/upd=false`), value-help/projection services, or other objects (PO/RFQ/PR/delivery/invoice). `ZSD_MCC_SO_MASS_UPDATE_SRV` (`/sap/opu/odata/sap/SD_MCC_SO_MASS_UPDATE_SRV`) **is** reachable with our user/auth (metadata GET 200 — it has a System Alias) and exposes Incoterms on `SlsOrder`/`SlsOrdItem`, but both entity sets are **`updatable=false`** (read-only preview); the only writable entities are `SlsDocReq`/`SlsDocReadReq`, keyed by a generated `SlsDocMassChangeRequestUUID` — i.e. the stateful draft "Mass Change Sales Documents" request flow, no FunctionImports.
   - **Decision 2026-10-08: Path A chosen, C interim, B rejected.** **A** = re-point create to `API_SALES_ORDER_SRV` (`A_SalesOrder` deep insert with `IncotermsClassification`/`IncotermsTransferLocation`/`IncotermsLocation1/2`) — the only clean, atomic per-order Incoterms route; pursued once the alias is granted. **C** (customer-master defaulting, Changes Log 2026-10-08) runs as the interim so VA03 is not blank meanwhile. **B** (keep LORD create, set Incoterms via mass-update) **rejected**: `SlsOrder` is not PATCHable, only the heavyweight stateful `SlsDocReq` draft flow is, and a create-then-update sequence leaves an order in SAP **without** Incoterms if step 2 fails.
   - **Post-alias runbook (doc-only, no code): `docs/incoterms-create-post-alias-runbook.md`** — re-verify metadata, one proof POST + VA03/VBKD read-back, the exact-files change plan (view / service.cds / mapper / adapter) preserving ContactPerson/PaymentTerms/HeaderPartner/deep-item, a feature-flag rollback keeping LORD as fallback, and the order-type/sales-area test matrix.
   - **Blocker (unchanged): Basis to assign System Alias `LOCAL` to `ZAPI_SALES_ORDER_SRV_0001` in `/IWFND/MAINT_SERVICE`.** Status: **Blocked on Basis; Path A decided, runbook ready, C interim active**.

## Changes Log

### 2026-10-08 — Create Sales Order: Incoterms-missing-in-VA03 root cause re-confirmed (read-only) + resolution path chosen (customer-master defaulting, no code)
- **Request.** Incoterms (VBKD-INCO1/INCO2_L) blank in VA03 for orders created via `#/sd/sales-orders/create`. Find root cause and fix.
- **Root cause (re-confirmed across all layers, matches Unresolved Issue 10(a)).** Incoterms is never captured, mapped, or posted, **and the create service cannot carry it**:
  - UI: `app/fiori-app/webapp/modules/sd/sales-order/view/CreateSalesOrder.view.xml` has no Incoterms input (binds PaymentTerms, ReqDeliveryDate, ContactPerson only).
  - CAP contract: `srv/sd/sales-order/service.cds` `OrderHeader` type (lines 94–117) declares no Incoterms field → CAP drops any undeclared field.
  - Mapper: `srv/integration/s4hana/sd/sales-inquiry/SalesInquiryMapper.js` `mapToS4OrderPayload` (133–167) uses a fixed whitelist; passthrough extras are only `['CustomerGroup2','PortOfLoading','PortOfDischarge','ContactPerson']`.
  - Adapter: `SalesInquiryAdapter.js` `headerPayload` (1387–1462) POSTs to `LORD_ODATA_ORDER_SRV/HeaderSet` (1468); extension loop iterates `INQUIRY_EXTENSION_FIELDS` (line 16) — no Incoterms.
  - Backend service: create goes through `/sap/opu/odata/sap/LORD_ODATA_ORDER_SRV`; its `Header` entity has **zero** Incoterms properties (so VBKD-INCO1/INCO2 can only come from SAP defaulting).
- **Resolution chosen by user (2026-10-08): SAP master-data defaulting — no repo change.** Maintain INCO1/INCO2(_L) (and INCOV if versioned) on the **sold-to** customer master (`BP`/`XD02`/`VD02` → matching Sales Area → Billing tab) for the order's exact sales area; standard SD defaults VBKD at creation. Verify by creating a **new** order (existing orders not updated retroactively) → VA03 Header → Sales/Billing tab, or `SE16N` on VBKD.
- **Files changed.** None (resolution is SAP master data, not code).
- **Commands run (read-only investigation only; no validation/tests, because no code changed; no SAP data modified — only repo-local metadata read).** `grep -ri incoterm` across repo; read of handler/service.cds/mapper/adapter; `grep -ic incoterm docs/sap-metadata-reference/LORD_ODATA_ORDER_SRV.edmx` → **0**; listed `Header` entity properties (confirmed `PaymentTermCode` present, no Incoterms); confirmed `API_SALES_ORDER_SRV` id present in `docs/sap-metadata-reference/sap_all_services.json` but its `$metadata` is **not** captured in the repo.
- **Blockers / risk.** Defaulting pulls from the **sold-to** for the **matching sales area** in standard config; a Z order type or a defaulting BAdI/user-exit could suppress it, and whether this system defaults from sold-to vs ship-to is unverified here. If master data alone does not fill it, the explicit-entry alternative (re-point create to `API_SALES_ORDER_SRV`, which exposes `IncotermsClassification`/`IncotermsTransferLocation`) is a large, verification-gated change needing live `$metadata` + a proven POST first (none of which could be run in this environment).
- **Next recommended action.** Maintain Incoterms on the sold-to customer master for the test sales area, create one new order, confirm INCO1/INCO2_L populated in VA03/VBKD; if blank, verify the order type's defaulting (sold-to vs ship-to, Z-type/BAdI) before considering the `API_SALES_ORDER_SRV` route.

### 2026-10-08 — Movement Type 261 attempt log: opt-in re-claim after a provably-not-posted attempt (post + reverse)
- **Change.** `GoodsIssueAttemptStore.createOrGet(data, reclaim)` gained an **opt-in** re-claim policy. When a caller passes `{ reclaimableStatuses: [...] }` and the latest attempt for the same identity (`RequestHash`) is in one of those statuses, a **new** attempt is created instead of returning the old one (409). The base `ReferenceDocument` gets a trailing **generation counter** (count of prior attempts for the identity), so the old row is kept (audit trail) and the new one has a distinct 16-char reference. The 201 flow passes **no** policy and keeps its unchanged idempotent-replay behaviour. New helper `_attemptsByRequestHash` groups generations by identity.
- **Post (`srv/wm/mvt261/service.js` postGoodsIssue).** Key is now a stable base `GI`+12-hex of `261|reservation|item|openQty`; policy `['rejected','not_posted']` — a definite SAP refusal (`rejected`) or a recheck-confirmed non-post (`not_posted`) is re-claimable, so fixing the cause and retrying works; a live/done attempt (`sending`/`unconfirmed`/`posted`/`delivery_created`) still 409s. `refDoc` for SAP is the claimed base+generation.
- **Reverse (same file, reverse action).** Base `RV`+12-hex of `262|doc|year|item`; policy `['rejected']` **only**. `not_posted` is deliberately **excluded** for reversals: the shared recheck looks a reversal up by our `RV…` key, but SAP's `CancelItem` stamps the 262 with the **original 261's** ReferenceDocument, so a reversal that actually posted can be mislabelled `not_posted`; auto-re-claiming it would risk a **double reversal**. A stuck reversal is cleared only via the QA_261.md runbook after an MB51 check.
- **Atomicity.** Two concurrent re-claims compute the same next reference; the `@assert.unique` ReferenceDocument claim lets exactly one win (same mechanism the 201 path uses). The common race — a re-claim already in flight (`sending`) — blocks the next one with 409, proven by test.
- **Recheck timer (ops).** The background recheck that ages `unconfirmed`→`not_posted` is started by `GoodsIssueService.init` whenever `NODE_ENV !== 'test'` and `GI_RECHECK_INTERVAL_MS` > 0, so it runs in the deployed QA/prod app (ops prerequisite: do not start QA/prod with `NODE_ENV=test` or interval 0). It confirms 201 outcomes via the 201 adapter; a **261/262-aware** SAP confirmation remains **deferred** (for 261/262 trust MB51, not the status).
- **Docs.** `QA_261.md` gained MUST-RUN rows #24 (post rejected→fix→retry) and #25 (reversal rejected→reopen→retry), a *Re-claim behaviour* section, the recheck-timer ops note, and a *Runbook — clearing a stuck posting attempt* (MB51 first; only a DBA on an approved ticket may delete a row; log the reference + MB51 finding + approver).
- **Tests.** `test/unit/wm/mvt261Scan.test.js` +6: rejected post→retry posts; not_posted 261→retry posts; posted→409; re-claim-in-flight→409 (exactly one wins); rejected reversal→retry posts; not_posted reversal→still 409. Existing cases still green: unconfirmed→409 (A6), sequential partials allowed (F3), concurrent posts→one 409 (F3).
- **Validation (all run 2026-10-08, all pass).** `npx jest test/unit/wm/mvt261Scan.test.js` 41/41; full `npx jest` **143 suites / 2379 tests**; `npx cds compile srv` OK; `npx cds build` completed (only pre-existing handling-unit/tr-to element-naming warnings, unrelated); `cd app/fiori-app && npx ui5lint` 0 findings; `git diff --check` clean; transient `gen/` removed (gitignored). Guard test `noGiQueueGuard` 2/2. **No live SAP data was modified** (all write paths mock-tested; only read-only calls ever hit SAP).

### 2026-10-08 — Movement Type 261: root-cause investigation of RESB.LGORT=PT01 vs PRVBE=IP05 (read-only) + scan-screen hardening

- **Scope:** Read-only root-cause analysis of why reservation 518006/3 (order 1002747, component 1000000653, batch IN25072189) issues from the empty PT01 while its supply area IP05 and stock are in CS01/W13. No SAP writes; write path and `checkStorageUnit` behavior unchanged.
- **Read-only evidence (RFC_READ_TABLE):**
  - **Order / BOM / version are identical** for the PT01-era and CS01-era orders: AFKO for orders 1002747 (PT01), 1000184 (CS01) and 1002737 (PT01) all use **BOM `00000023` alt `01` type M usage 1**, header material 3000000155, **production version TACA**. So BOM and version are **not** the differentiator.
  - **BOM item (`STPO`)** for component 1000000653 in BOM 00000023: **`LGORT` blank, `PRVBE` blank** — the BOM item does not drive the storage location or supply area.
  - **Material master (`MARC`, plant 1120)** for 1000000653: `LGPRO`/`LGFSB`/`VSPVB` all **blank** (`DISGR=Z120`). Across the sibling IP05 components of the same order, `MARC-LGPRO` is blank for ones that got **both** PT01 and CS01, and where it *is* set (8300000265 → `LGPRO=PT01`) the reservation went to **CS01** — the opposite. So **`MARC-LGPRO` does not explain the reservation LGORT** either.
  - **Within one order (reservation 518006)** the 7 components split PT01/CS01 under the **same PRVBE IP05**: items 1/3/4/8 → PT01 (non-WM, blank LGNUM); items 2/6 → CS01 (blank LGNUM); items 5/7 → CS01 with **LGNUM W01 / LGTYP IP5** (WM). Determination is therefore **per component**, not order/BOM/version level.
  - **`T320` plant 1120:** `CS01 → W01` (and CS02/FG01/… → W01); **`PT01` is absent → not warehouse-managed.** Yet the 1000000653 stock quant (`LQUA`) sits in **CS01 / W13**, and the WM reservations (items 5/7) show **W01**, while the IP05 control cycles (`PKHD`) all reference **W13** → the storage-location⇄warehouse assignment is itself inconsistent (W01 vs W13 for the same material/plant).
- **Root cause (what is verified):** `RESB.LGORT` for this component is **not derivable from any current component master data** — BOM item (blank), production version (identical), and `MARC-LGPRO` (blank, or contradictory where set) all fail to explain PT01. The value was captured at order creation from master data/customizing that has since changed (reservations are not re-derived), over an inconsistent storage-location/warehouse setup for 1000000653 in plant 1120 (non-WM PT01 as issue location vs WM supply area IP05 → CS01, and CS01 mapped to W01 in T320 but stock/control-cycle in W13).
- **Could NOT verify read-only (stated explicitly):** the exact historical driver (change documents `CDHDR`/`CDPOS` for `MARC-LGPRO` and other MRP2 fields), the storage-location determination customizing (**OPJ8**), and the warehouse-reorganization history (T320 W13→W01). These are config / change-log, not table data reachable via RFC_READ_TABLE here. `MKAL` could not be read (field-name mismatch on this release).
- **App hardening (code, safe, this turn):** `test/unit/wm/mvt261Adapter.test.js` — strengthened the WM-case scanContext test to assert the supply-area unit (different storage location, same warehouse) stays `Status:'Available', Suggested:true` and `SupplyAreaStock:0` (behavior unchanged); the non-WM case (CS01 unit `OnHold`/`inSupplyArea`/not suggested, `SupplyAreaStock` populated) is covered by the test added in the prior entry. Dynamic warning-strip text (`scan261SupplyAreaStock` with qty / unit / supply-area / issue-location placeholders) and `scan261Reject_inSupplyArea` are present in both i18n bundles (`i18n.properties`, `i18n_en.properties` — the only locales). Scan list ordering is strict FIFO (oldest GR date first) with consumable units flagged `Suggested`; when units exist but none are issuable they are shown flagged (stranded stock visible), and the "No production stock for this order" empty-state shows only when the list is truly empty — confirmed sensible, no change needed.
- **Verification:** `npx jest test/unit/wm` 52 suites / 1071 tests, `npx cds compile srv/wm/mvt261/service.cds`, `ui5lint` (0 findings), `git diff --check` — all pass. Live read-only `scanContext(518006,3)` unchanged from prior entry (CS01 unit OnHold/inSupplyArea/not suggested, SupplyAreaStock 25).
- **SAP fix (for a functional consultant):** align the issue storage location of component 1000000653 (plant 1120) with its WM supply area. Two options — (a) **operational:** MIGO **311** (or **LT01** if WM-processed) transfer 25 KG batch IN25072189 **CS01 → PT01**, then run the 261 PGI; (b) **config/master data:** set the component's production/issue storage location to a warehouse-managed location consistent with IP05 (CS01) via **MM02** MRP2 `LGPRO` (or the **CS03** BOM item SLoc, or the **C223** production version), review **OPJ8** storage-location determination, and reconcile the **T320** storage-location⇄warehouse assignment (CS01 currently W01 vs stock/control-cycle W13). T-codes to inspect: **CO03** (order/reservation), **CS03** (BOM item), **C223** (production version), **MM03** (MRP2 / WM views), **MMBE** / **LX02** (stock by location/warehouse), **OPJ8** (SLoc determination), **PKMC** (control cycle PKHD 0007233).

### 2026-10-08 — Movement Type 261 Scan screen: supply-area stock shown honestly + investigation of the PT01/CS01 stock discrepancy

- **Symptom:** `wm/mvt261/open/518006/3` → post error `Goods issue not possible: unrestricted stock 0 KG is less than 25 KG`, although 25 KG visibly exists.
- **Investigation (live, read-only):** Material 1000000653, batch-managed, open 25 KG. Reservation 518006/3: `LGORT = PT01`, `LGNUM/LGTYP` blank (**non-WM**), `PRVBE = IP05`. `MARD`: PT01 = 0, **CS01 = 25**; `MCHB` batch IN25072189: CS01 = 25, PT01 = 0; `LQUA`: 25 KG in CS01 / W13 (SU 1000048321). `PVBE(1120, IP05).LGORT = CS01` (the supply area). `MARC.LGPRO` empty. `PKHD`: 7 control cycles for the material, IP05 → warehouse W13 / staging type IP5. Historical `RESB.LGORT` flipped over time: older order 1000184 = CS01/W13/IP5 (WM), recent orders 1002737/1002747 = PT01 (non-WM). Past 261s posted from both CS01 and PT01; PT01 was drained, leaving 25 KG stranded in CS01. **Root cause = SAP master-data inconsistency** (reservation carries a WM-PP supply area IP05/W13 but a non-WM storage location PT01; stock stranded in CS01). The app's `0 stock` for PT01 is correct; this is not a code bug and must be fixed in SAP (transfer CS01→PT01, or correct the component storage-location determination).
- **App-side fix (read/display only; no write-path change):**
  - `srv/integration/s4hana/wm/Mvt261Adapter.js` `scanContext()` — a unit is now `Suggested` only when issuable by this reservation (`c.Warehouse ? true : q.LGORT === reservation LGORT`). Supply-area stock in another location of a non-WM reservation is shown but flagged `Status:'OnHold'`, `Reason:'inSupplyArea'` (Value1 = PSA location, Value2 = issue location), never suggested. Added `SupplyAreaStock` (qty in the PSA location when it differs from the non-WM issue location).
  - `srv/wm/mvt261/service.cds` — `ScanContext` gains `SupplyAreaStock : Decimal(13,3)`.
  - `app/fiori-app/webapp/modules/wm/mvt261/view/Scan261.view.xml` — Warning strip `scan261SupplyArea` shown when `SupplyAreaStock > 0`.
  - `Scan261.controller.js` — `formatMessage(sKey, v1..v4)` helper for the parameterized strip.
  - i18n (both bundles) — `scan261Reject_inSupplyArea`, `scan261SupplyAreaStock`.
  - **`checkStorageUnit` intentionally unchanged:** a manual scan of a different storage location keeps the existing warn-but-accept behavior (test T7); a first attempt at a hard `wrongStorageLocation` reject was reverted because it broke that intended behavior and the display fix already removes the confusion.
  - `test/unit/wm/mvt261Adapter.test.js` — new `scanContext` case: non-WM reservation (PT01, PSA CS01) → the CS01 unit is `OnHold`/`inSupplyArea`/not suggested and `SupplyAreaStock` = 25.
- **Verification:** live read-only `scanContext(518006,3)` → `SupplyAreaStock 25`, unit 1000048321 `Status OnHold, Suggested false, Reason inSupplyArea (CS01/PT01)`. `npx jest test/unit/wm` 52 suites / 1071 tests, `npx cds compile`, `ui5lint` (0 findings), `git diff --check` — all pass.
- **What to check in SAP:** MMBE/LX02 material 1000000653 plant 1120 (CS01 25 vs PT01 0, batch IN25072189); CO03 order 1002747 component 1000000653 (LGORT PT01 vs supply area IP05); why `RESB.LGORT = PT01` while `PRVBE = IP05` maps to W13 (BOM item SLoc / production version / MRP2). Resolve by transfer CS01→PT01 or by correcting the determination.

### 2026-10-08 — Movement Type 261 Scan screen: warehouse mismatch suppresses the derivative staging shortfall

- **Symptom (scan screen):** `Scanning is not possible: transmitted warehouse number is W13; determined warehouse number is W01; available stock shortfall of 370 KG in interim storage bin IP5/0001002619`.
- **Root cause:** `cycle()` reported two blockers at once. The warehouse mismatch (`RESB-LGNUM` W13 ≠ `T320` W01) is the real, actionable blocker. The staging-shortfall line was computed with `stagingWarehouse = configuredWarehouse || resb.LGNUM` = W01 (the *determined* warehouse), so it looked for staged stock in W01 while the reservation points at W13, found none, and reported the full 370 KG as a shortfall — a derivative, misleading consequence of the mismatch.
- **Change (`srv/integration/s4hana/wm/Mvt261Adapter.js`):** `cycle()` now computes `warehouseMismatch = Boolean(resb.LGNUM) && (configuredWarehouse ? resb.LGNUM !== configuredWarehouse : true)` (the same condition `blockers()` uses to raise the mismatch) and sets `stagingShortfall = … && !warehouseMismatch`. A mismatched item therefore reports only the warehouse-mismatch reason, the `WmStaging` step carries no shortfall text, and the `StagingShortfall` field is 0. Once the warehouse is aligned the staging check runs and reports a genuine shortfall again. `openItems()` (the worklist) is unchanged — it does not surface the warehouse-mismatch reason and its `_stagingShortfalls` already evaluates against the reservation's own `LGNUM`.
- **Test (`test/unit/wm/mvt261Adapter.test.js`):** new `cycle` case — `RESB-LGNUM W13`, `T320 W01`, staging type `IP5`, open 370 KG, no staged LQUA → GoodsIssue reason is exactly `transmitted warehouse number is W13; determined warehouse number is W01`, contains no `available stock shortfall`, and `StagingShortfall` is 0.
- **Verification:** live read-only, order 1002619 / reservation 479284 items 3 & 4 → block reason now `transmitted warehouse number is W13; determined warehouse number is W01` alone (staging line gone). `npx jest test/unit/wm` 52 suites / 1070 tests, `git diff --check` — pass.

### 2026-10-08 — Movement Type 261 Scan screen: stock list restricted to production-related stock (reservation + PSA location, reserved batch, REL gate)

- **Request:** On `#/wm/mvt261/open/{reservation}/{item}` (route params = reservation + item; e.g. `519944/6`, order `1002760`), the PGI stock list must show only production-related stock for the order, not all plant stock. Modify in place; no parallel screen.
- **STEP 1 findings:** The route is the per-item `Scan261` screen; its stock list (`scanContext.Units`, LQUA storage units) was already scoped to the one component's material + the order's plant + the reservation's storage location/warehouse — not "all plant stock". Genuine gaps vs the requested rules: (4) batch — the list never restricted to `RESB.CHARG`; (3) production supply area location — not read at all; (5) stock type — QI/blocked/returns/in-TO were shown flagged (kept, per user decision); (6) plantScope — mvt261 enforces none (handling-unit does). Decisions taken (AskUserQuestion): keep per-item/in place; keep non-unrestricted stock shown-but-flagged; include the PSA location (discover its source first); leave plantScope as a noted gap.
- **SAP discovery (read-only, live, AGENTS.md §SAP):** reservation `519944/6` → RESB `MATNR 1000000318`, `WERKS 1130`, `LGORT CS02`, `CHARG ''` (no batch), `PRVBE IP02-2F-HU`, `LGTYP 2FL`, order `1002760`. `DD03L` confirmed `PVBE` has `WERKS,PRVBE,LGORT` (no LGTYP/LGPLA; bin is `LGBZO`). `PVBE(1130, IP02-2F-HU).LGORT = CS02`. So PSA location source = `RESB.PRVBE → PVBE(WERKS,PRVBE).LGORT`.
- **Change:**
  - `srv/integration/s4hana/wm/Mvt261Adapter.js` — `cycle()` reads `RESB.PRVBE`, reads one `PVBE` row when PRVBE is set, and returns `SupplyArea`/`SupplyAreaStorageLocation`. `scanContext()` now builds `locations = {reservation LGORT ∪ PSA LGORT}` and filters LQUA by `LGORT ∈ locations AND (no warehouse | LGNUM = warehouse) AND (no reserved batch | CHARG = reserved batch)`; computes `released = OrderStatus includes 'REL'` and returns an empty `Units` list for a non-released order; exposes `Batch`, `SupplyArea`, `SupplyAreaStorageLocation`, `OrderReleased`. `checkStorageUnit()` rejects a scanned unit whose batch ≠ the reserved batch with reason `wrongBatch`.
  - `srv/wm/mvt261/service.cds` — `Cycle261` gains `SupplyArea`/`SupplyAreaStorageLocation`; `ScanContext` gains `Batch`/`SupplyArea`/`SupplyAreaStorageLocation`/`OrderReleased`.
  - `app/fiori-app/webapp/modules/wm/mvt261/view/Scan261.view.xml` — info strip "Only components of this production order are shown…" (+ reserved batch when set) for released orders; a separate Information strip for non-released orders; units table header now shows the open qty; empty-state text `scan261NoProdStock` ("No production stock for this order").
  - `app/fiori-app/webapp/i18n/i18n.properties` and `i18n_en.properties` — `scan261Reject_wrongBatch`, `scan261NoProdStock`, `scan261ProdStockInfo`, `scan261ProdStockBatch`, `scan261OrderNotReleased`.
  - `test/unit/wm/mvt261Adapter.test.js` — new `scanContext` cases (reservation+PSA locations included / unrelated LGORT excluded; reserved-batch-only; non-released → empty list + `OrderReleased:false`) and a `checkStorageUnit` `wrongBatch` case.
- **Filter rules as implemented (server-side, one batched LQUA read; no per-row loop; paging unchanged):** a stock (storage-unit) row shows iff material = reservation's AND `LGORT ∈ {reservation LGORT, PVBE PSA LGORT}` AND (warehouse unset OR `LGNUM` = order warehouse) AND (reservation batch unset OR `CHARG` = reservation batch) AND order is REL. Non-unrestricted quants remain visible but flagged `OnHold`/`Blocked` and never `Suggested`. Open qty for the component is shown in the header and the units-table title.
- **Verification (measured, read-only; nothing posted):**
  1. Live `scanContext(519944,6)`: `OrderReleased true`, `SupplyArea IP02-2F-HU`, `SupplyAreaStorageLocation CS02`, `Batch ''`, warehouse `W12`. LQUA returned 22 SUs of the material in W12; **21 in CS02 shown, the 1 in CS01 (`2000039789`, 2 KG) excluded** → location filter proven. The single `Available`/`Suggested` row is the 677 KG in the PSA staging bin (`2FL`/`0001002760`); `OH1/ONHOLD` rows shown flagged `OnHold`, not suggested.
  2. Material not in the order / in another location: excluded (list keyed to the reservation's material; CS01 exclusion demonstrates cross-location filtering).
  3. Remaining qty 0 / deleted / final-issued: blocked by existing `cycle()` logic (unchanged).
  4. Batch-specific reservation: unit test proves only the reserved batch is listed (this live item has no reserved batch, so N/A live).
  5. plantScope A/B isolation: **N/A** — not enforced in mvt261 (recorded gap).
  6. Non-REL order: unit test proves empty list + info message.
  7. PGI post for a shown row: **NOT executed** (needs the user's explicit OK; sandbox order only).
  8. Cost: `scanContext` adds exactly one `PVBE` read (only when `PRVBE` is set); no duplicate requests. Browser load-time/console check still pending (needs a `cds watch` with the user's SAP login).
  9. `npx jest test/unit/wm` 52 suites / 1068 tests, `npx cds compile srv/wm/mvt261/service.cds`, `ui5lint` (0 findings), `git diff --check` — all pass.
- **What to check in SAP:** CO03 order `1002760` status REL and its component `1000000318`; MMBE / LX02 stock for `1000000318` in plant 1130 (CS02 vs CS01) and the PSA bin `2FL/0001002760`; MB51 after any (future, approved) 261 posting.

### 2026-10-08 — Movement Type 261 Open Items: interim-staging shortfall items classified Blocked (list ↔ scan page consistency)

- **Symptom:** An item showing `Scanning is not possible: available stock shortfall of 712 KG in interim storage bin IP1/0001002819` on the scan page appeared in the **Open** tab of the Open-261 worklist IconTabBar, not **Blocked** — the same item classified differently in the list vs when opened.
- **Root cause:** `Mvt261Adapter.openItems()` (the list) computes `ScanPossible`/`Blocked`/`BlockReason` from order status, `MovementAllowed`, order release, order-status blockers and ready storage units (`_readyStorageUnits`, material|plant), but never evaluates the **WM-PP interim-staging shortfall** that `cycle()`/`scanContext`/`checkStorageUnit`/`postGoodsIssue` all enforce (reservation item with `RESB-LGTYP` set whose staged `LQUA` stock in `LGTYP`/bin < open quantity). So a staging-short item was marked scannable in the list and only blocked once the scan page ran the cycle.
- **Change (`srv/integration/s4hana/wm/Mvt261Adapter.js`, read-only):**
  - Added `_stagingShortfalls(items)`: for the listed items it reads `RESB` (`WERKS`, `BWART='261'`, not deleted/issued, `LGTYP <> ''`) once per distinct plant to find staged items and their `LGTYP`/`LGPLA`/`AUFNR`/`LGNUM`, then reads `LQUA` (`VERME > 0`) once per plant and sums staged stock by `matnr|werks|lgtyp|bin` and warehouse. For each staged item, `bin = RESB-LGPLA || order.padStart(10)` and `warehouse = RESB-LGNUM` (cycle's fallback); when `staged < open` it returns the reason string identical to `cycle()` line 359: `available stock shortfall of <qty> <unit> in interim storage bin <type>/<bin>`. Returns `{}` when no listed item needs staging (skips the LQUA read). Two batch reads per distinct plant, not one per item.
  - `openItems()` calls `_stagingShortfalls(Items)` alongside `_orderStatuses`/`_readyStorageUnits` and adds the shortfall as a block reason, placed **before** the "No ready storage units in stock" heuristic so the exact scan-page message wins for staged items.
- **No CDS/UI change:** `Open261Item.Blocked`/`BlockReason`/`ScanPossible` and the Open/Blocked/All IconTabBar already consume these fields; the list now simply computes them correctly.
- **Tests (`test/unit/wm/mvt261Adapter.test.js`):** two new `openItems` cases — a staged item short in `IP1/0001000086` (staged 488, open 1000) resolves `{ ScanPossible:false, Blocked:true, BlockReason:'available stock shortfall of 512 KG in interim storage bin IP1/0001000086' }`; the same item with 1200 staged resolves `{ ScanPossible:true, Blocked:false, BlockReason:'' }` (proving a ready SU count alone no longer hides a staging shortfall).
- **Validation (all pass):** `npx jest test/unit/wm` → 52 suites / 1064 tests; `git diff --check` → clean.

### 2026-10-08 — Movement Type 261 Goods Issue: WM-managed location returns outbound delivery instead of a false 502

- **Symptom:** UI `POST /odata/v4/mvt261/postGoodsIssue` → `502 Bad Gateway`; backend message `SAP did not return a material document for the goods issue. sap-message: {"code":"L9/514","message":"Delivery 80000087 created","severity":"success",...}`.
- **Root cause:** The reservation item's storage location is WM-managed. SAP answers the `API_MATERIAL_DOCUMENT_SRV/A_MaterialDocumentHeader` 261 POST with HTTP 201, an **empty** `MaterialDocument`, and `sap-message L9/514` because it converts the goods movement into an **outbound delivery** (`80000087`); the material document and the actual stock issue exist only once **PGI** is posted for that delivery. `Mvt261Adapter.postGoodsIssue` (line 626) threw `httpError(502, ...)` on the empty material document — a false failure on a successful SAP outcome. The sibling 201 path (`GoodsIssuePostingClient._submitMaterialDocument`) already handled this exact case gracefully.
- **Change (root-cause, reuses the proven 201 helpers; no new SAP write):**
  - `srv/integration/s4hana/wm/Mvt261Adapter.js`: require `GoodsIssuePostingClient`; `_post` now also returns `headers`; `postGoodsIssue` on empty material document parses `sap-message` via `GoodsIssuePostingClient.parseSapMessage(headers)` → `deliveryFromSapMessage(...)` and, when an L9/514 delivery is present, returns `{ MaterialDocument:'', MaterialDocumentYear:'', DeliveryNumber, Pending:true, SapMessage, Message }` instead of throwing. A truly empty response with no delivery still throws 502; genuine SAP errors (403/400/etc.) still propagate.
  - `srv/wm/mvt261/service.cds`: `MaterialDocumentResult` extended with `DeliveryNumber : String(10)`, `Pending : Boolean`, `Message : String`.
  - `srv/wm/mvt261/service.js`: a `Pending` result records attempt status `not_posted` carrying `DeliveryNumber` (not `posted`, because no goods issue posted yet); result passed through unchanged.
  - `app/fiori-app/webapp/modules/wm/mvt261/controller/Scan261.controller.js`: `onPost` success branch shows a warning with the delivery number + "PGI required" note (`oRes.Message` / new i18n key `scan261PostDelivery`) for the pending-delivery case, instead of the misleading "material document posted" success.
  - `app/fiori-app/webapp/i18n/i18n.properties` and `i18n_en.properties`: added `scan261PostDelivery`.
  - `test/unit/wm/mvt261Adapter.test.js`: replaced the old assertion (L9/514 → 502) with two tests — L9/514 JSON delivery → resolves `{ DeliveryNumber:'0080000087', Pending:true }`, and neither material document nor delivery → still 502; plus the existing 403 propagation.

  - `checkStorageUnit()`: validated scanned storage unit quants against `configuredWarehouse`. Rejects with `wrongWarehouse` (`Value1: quants[0].LGNUM`, `Value2: configuredWarehouse`) if scanned SU quants belong to a different warehouse than the storage location.
  - `service.cds`: added `Warehouse : String(3)` to `type ScanContext`; updated `type ScanResult` Reason comment to document `wrongWarehouse`.

  - `i18n.properties` & `i18n_en.properties`: added `scan261Reject_wrongWarehouse=Wrong warehouse: storage unit is in warehouse {0}, storage location requires warehouse {1}.`

### 2026-10-07 17:45 IST — Movement Type 261: Root Cause & Resolution of Warehouse Mismatch (W13 vs W01)

Investigated SAP error `HTTP 400 - Transmitted warehouse number is W13; determined warehouse number is W01` on reservation `418011/2`:

- **Root Cause Analysis:**
  - Production Order `1002034` / Reservation `418011` was stored with `RESB-LGNUM = 'W13'`.
  - Configuration table `T320` maps Plant `1120` / SLoc `CS01` to Warehouse `W01`.
  - During posting, SAP's standard material document engine compares the reservation's transmitted warehouse number (`W13`) against the determined storage location warehouse number (`W01`), rejecting the post with HTTP 400.
- **Resolution & Fully Aligned Candidate:**
  - Scanned for reservation items where `RESB-LGNUM == T320-LGNUM`.
  - Discovered **`490788/2`**: Order `1002668` (REL), Material `1000000159` (exact same material), Plant `1120`, SLoc `CS01`.
    - `RESB-LGNUM = 'W01'` matches `T320-LGNUM = 'W01'` (0 mismatch).
    - `LQUA` in interim bin `IP5 / 0001002668` (Warehouse `W01`) holds `2600.000 KG` with batch **`IN26001003`**.
    - `MCHB` unrestricted stock has `10,400.000 KG` for batch `IN26001003`.
    - All cycle steps (`Availability`, `WmStaging`, `Reservation`, `ProductionOrder`) are `done`, `GoodsIssue: open`.
  - Also identified **`490788/4`** (`1000000318`, 2145 KG, batch `IN26001010` in `IP5/0001002668` W01) and **`520615/1`** (`3000000415`, 100 KG, batch `INWS260004` in `GFL/0002000623` W12).
- **Validation:**
  - Verified `T320`, `RESB-LGNUM`, and `LQUA` records over RFC; `git diff --check` clean.

Investigated the SAP posting rejection `HTTP 400 - Available stock shortfall of 480.000 KG in interim storage bin` on reservation `480960/1` and scanned all open 261 reservation items across SAP S/4HANA (client 220) to identify actually postable items.

    - `109653/2`: Order `4000023081` (REL), Material `8000000117`, Plant `1120`, SLoc `HS01`, Open `1 KG`, Stock `104.1 KG`.
  - **Category B: Pre-Staged WM Items (Stock already confirmed in interim supply bin in `LQUA`):**
    - `418011/2`: Order `1002034` (REL), Material `1000000159`, Plant `1120`, SLoc `CS01`, Open `2600 KG`. Staged quant in `LQUA`: Storage Type `IP5`, Bin `0001002034` has `2600.000 KG` batch `IN26000261`. MCHB unrestricted batch stock is `19,200.000 KG`. All cycle steps `done`.

    - `490788/4`: Order `1002668` (REL), Material `1000000318`, Plant `1120`, SLoc `CS01`, Open `2145 KG`. Staged quant in `LQUA`: Storage Type `IP5`, Bin `0001002668` has `2145.000 KG` batch `IN25003444`. All cycle steps `done`.
    - `515085/1` to `515085/5`: Order `1002710` (REL), Plant `1110`, SLoc `CS01`. Components `1000000507`, `1000000187`, `1000000315`, `1000000634`, `1000000427` fully staged in interim bin `IP1 / 0001002710`.

### 2026-10-07 17:15 IST — Goods Issue 261: Stage 2 UI posting capability & idempotency guard

Implemented Stage 2 for Goods Issue Movement Type 261, enabling the UI Post action on route `#/wm/mvt261/open/{reservation}/{item}` with CAP service binding and idempotency protection.

- **Changes & Implementation:**
  - **CAP Service Action (`srv/wm/mvt261/service.cds`):** Added `MaterialDocumentResult` type (`MaterialDocument`, `MaterialDocumentYear`, `SapMessage`) and action `postGoodsIssue(reservation, item, quantity, batch) returns MaterialDocumentResult` requiring roles Viewer, WarehouseClerk, WarehouseManager, Admin.
  - **Idempotency Guard & Handler (`srv/wm/mvt261/service.js`):** Bound action `postGoodsIssue` to `GoodsIssueAttemptStore`. Generates a unique 16-char header `ReferenceDocument`, validates input, checks `hasOpenAttemptForReservation(reservation, item)` to reject concurrent duplicate posts with HTTP 409, logs attempt as `sending`, executes `Mvt261Adapter.postGoodsIssue`, and records status as `posted` (or `rejected` on error).
  - **S/4 Adapter (`srv/integration/s4hana/wm/Mvt261Adapter.js`):** Passed `input.referenceDocument` through `postGoodsIssue` to `buildHeaderEnvelope` so the header `ReferenceDocument` is transmitted to `API_MATERIAL_DOCUMENT_SRV`.
  - **UI View (`Scan261.view.xml`):** Added `Button id="scan261Post"` (`type="Emphasized"`, `icon="sap-icon://paper-plane"`) in the scanned table headerToolbar, enabled only when scanned quantity is `Covered`, not busy, and item not blocked.
  - **UI Controller (`Scan261.controller.js`):** Added `MessageBox` import and `onPost` handler: confirms post with dialog, calls `ODataClient.post("/odata/v4/mvt261/postGoodsIssue", ...)`, shows success dialog displaying the SAP Material Document number, and navigates back to `wmOpen261`.
  - **i18n Bundles (`i18n.properties`, `i18n_en.properties`):** Added keys `scan261Post`, `scan261PostTitle`, `scan261PostConfirm`, `scan261PostSuccess`, `scan261PostFailed`.
  - **Unit Testing (`test/unit/wm/mvt261Scan.test.js`):** Updated scanning test assertions; added unit tests verifying the post button presence, controller `onPost` wiring, CAP action definition, input validation (400), and idempotency blocking of concurrent requests (409).

### 2026-10-07 16:35 IST — Handling Unit list: fixed text alignment, typographic baseline & divider layout in KPI strip

Investigated and resolved visual text alignment and separator issues in the compact Handling Units KPI count strip (`HandlingUnits.view.xml` lines 13-30) using Chrome DevTools MCP.

- **DevTools MCP Root Cause Inspection:**
  - **Issue 1 (Typographic Baseline Step):** Bounding-box vertical centering (`alignItems="Center"`) caused a 3.0px baseline discrepancy between small labels (`sapUiTinyText`, 12px font / 14px bounding box) and counts (`fontBold`, 14px font / 16px bounding box). Measured in live DOM: label baseline = 114.25px, text baseline = 117.25px, causing numbers to visually sag below labels.
  - **Issue 2 (Hanging Leading Pipes on Wrap):** Literal `<Text text="|" />` elements prepended to status items created orphan leading pipe characters on wrapped lines at mobile viewports (e.g., `| Planned 1,076` on line 2, `| In Transit 85` on line 3).
  - **Issue 3 (Asymmetric Separator Spacing):** Literal pipe items had `sapUiTinyMarginBegin` + `sapUiTinyMarginEnd` (8px + 8px = 16px left of pipe) versus 8px right of pipe before the label.
  - **Issue 4 (Repeater Flex Containment):** Inner `<HBox items="{huView>/kpis}">` was rendered as an outer block flex container, separating the Total count into a solitary row on narrow screens.
- **Implementation & Fixes:**
  - **Typographic Baseline Alignment:** Changed `alignItems` on `HBox id="huKpiRow"` and individual item HBoxes to `"Baseline"`. Added CSS `.huKpiStrip, .huKpiStrip .sapMHBox { align-items: baseline !important; }` and `.huKpiStrip .sapMFlexItem { display: inline-flex !important; align-items: baseline !important; }` to align font base lines mathematically across all UI5 flex wrappers.
  - **CSS Dividers & Clean Wrap:** Removed all literal `<Text text="|" />` elements. Added `.huKpiItem` with `border-right: 1px solid var(--sapContent_SeparatorColor, #d9d9d9)` and balanced padding/margin (0.625rem). Added `.huKpiItem:last-child { border-right: none !important; }`. Added `.huKpiStrip .huKpiGroup { display: contents !important; }` so all repeated status items participate directly as sibling flex items, cleanly wrapping without orphan separators.
- **DevTools MCP Verification & Measurements:**
  - **Desktop (1440px):**
    - Height: 24px (1.5rem), total with margin 32px (2.0rem).
    - Baselines: all 6 items share identical baseline y = 112.5px. Baseline diff = **0.0px** (was 3.0px).
    - Dividers: rendered vertically centered, 1px solid, clean spacing.
  - **Mobile (390px):**
    - Height: 32px (2.0rem), total with margin 40px (2.5rem).
    - Wrap: cleanly wraps into 2 rows of 3 items (Row 1: Total, Planned, Active; Row 2: Shipped, Deleted, In Transit).
    - Leading dividers: **zero hanging pipes**. Row 2 starts flush left at x = 8px.
    - Overflow: `hasHorizontalOverflow: false`.
- **Validation Commands:**
  - `npm --prefix app/fiori-app run lint`: 0 findings detected.
  - `npx jest test/unit/wm/handlingUnitsController.test.js test/unit/controller/uiConsistency.test.js`: 23/23 tests pass.
  - `npx jest test/unit/wm`: 51 suites / 1045 tests pass.
  - `git diff --check`: clean (0 errors).
- Files: `app/fiori-app/webapp/modules/wm/handling-unit/view/HandlingUnits.view.xml`, `app/fiori-app/webapp/css/style.css`.

Total item retains `id="huKpiTotal"`, tooltip `{i18n>huKpiTotalTooltip}`, label `{i18n>huKpiTotal}` with `class="sapUiTinyText sapUiTinyMarginEnd textMuted"`, bold count with `class="fontBold"`. Status items rendered via inner `sap.m.HBox items="{huView>/kpis}"` with `|` pipe separator (`class="textMuted sapUiTinyMarginBegin sapUiTinyMarginEnd"`), label `{huView>name}` (`sapUiTinyText textMuted`), and bold count (`fontBold`).

### 2026-10-07 — HU labels: snap the Code 128 barcode to whole printer dots (203 dpi) + odd-digit subset C

Made every narrow bar an integer number of printer dots so bar/space ratios don't distort when the printer rounds, improving scan reliability on cheap thermal scanners. Barcode-only change; `labels()`, chunking, plant scoping, size picker, typography/type scale, file names and jsPDF lazy-loading untouched.

- **STEP 1 (measured, before):** narrow bars were non-whole everywhere @203 dpi — A 3.41, B 6.83, E 6.54, C 10.17, D 13.80 dots (10-digit HU); worst A 20-digit 2.28 dots. Subset C was already used for **even**-length all-digit; **odd**-length fell back to all-Code-B (~2× modules).
- **STEP 2 — `Barcode.js`:** extended subset C to **all-digit strings of any length** — pairs in Code C, a trailing odd digit switches to Code B (new `CODE_B=100`), correct checksum/start/stop. zxing decoded **20/20** mixed HUs (even, odd, leading zeros `0012`/`000000`, 1-digit, alphanumeric) @300 dpi — value == input every time.
- **STEP 3 — dot snapping (`HuLabelLayout.js` + both renderers):** single config `TARGET_DPI=203` (allowed 203/300), `DOT_MM`, `MIN_DOTS={A:3,B:3,E:3,C:3,D:4}`. New shared `snapBarcode(L,totalModules)` → `narrowDots=floor(innerW/DOT_MM/total)`, `narrowMm=narrowDots*DOT_MM`, `widthMm=narrowMm*total` (≤innerW), left edge centred then snapped to a whole dot so **every** bar edge lands on a dot. Both `HuLabelPrinter.section` and `HuLabelPdf.drawLabel` use it (identical x/width). `fits()` now requires `narrowDots >= MIN_DOTS[size]` **and** `narrowMm >= minBarMm`; too dense reuses the existing `huLabelTooSmall` skip → nextLarger. Barcode height + vertical layout unchanged (barcode block just narrower, centred; other elements don't move).
- **Measured after (10-digit HU):** whole narrow bars A 3 / B 6 / E 6 / C 10 / D 13 dots; widths ≤ innerW, centred. **Bar-edge error = 0.0000 dots** on all 5 sizes (PDF rendered at exactly 203 and 300 dpi); zxing decode OK on all 5 at **both 203 and 300 dpi**; page sizes unchanged (A 141.7×70.9 … D 566.9×283.5 pt).
- **A-size limitation (reported, not silently relaxed):** with `MIN_DOTS=3`, **A now prints all-digit HUs up to 12 digits** (121 modules → 3 dots) at 203 dpi; a **20-digit HU no longer fits A** (165 modules → 2 dots) and routes to **B** (4 dots). Options if 20-digit on A is required: use B/E (next size), raise A's inner width, or accept sub-3-dot bars (not recommended). At 300 dpi A reaches further (more dots per mm).
- **Validation:** full `npx jest test/unit` **128 suites / 2230 tests**; `ui5lint` clean; `git diff --check` clean. Updated tests: odd-digit subset C encoding, dot-snapped `fits()` (A 12-digit ok / 20-digit → B), `snapBarcode` whole-dot invariants, buildHtml barcode width = snapped width.
- **Report-only:** A (3 dots) / D (4 dots) sit at their MIN_DOTS floor for a 10-digit HU — fine, but least margin.
- **Manual checklist:** on the real 203 dpi thermal printer, scan ≥10 labels per size (especially **A at 3 dots** and **D at 4 dots**); confirm a 12-digit HU scans on A and that a 20-digit HU correctly routes to B; verify the centred (narrower) barcode leaves adequate quiet zone on your stock.
- Files: `util/Barcode.js`, `util/HuLabelLayout.js`, `util/HuLabelPrinter.js`, `util/HuLabelPdf.js`, `test/unit/wm/huLabelLayout.test.js`, `test/unit/wm/handlingUnitLabel.test.js`.

### 2026-10-07 — HU labels: typographic hierarchy + font-size overhaul (all 5 sizes), one shared wrap engine

Replaced height-only font scaling with a deliberate 4-level hierarchy and a stack-based vertical rhythm, driven from the one shared spec so print (HTML) and PDF stay identical. Barcode, `labels()`, chunking, plant scoping, the size picker, `minBarMm`, file names and jsPDF lazy-loading untouched (verified).

- **Spec rewrite** (`util/HuLabelLayout.js`): removed the `R` ratio table + `fontPt()`; added explicit per-size `TYPE` scale (pt + weight per role) and a **stack model** that computes yTop positions (material → barcode → HU number → meta), spreading leftover evenly into the two flexible gaps (HU hugs the barcode via a 1.0 mm tight gap), bottom slack = pad. Added a shared **Helvetica/Helvetica-Bold AFM** text engine — `measureMm` / `wrap` / `ellipsize` / `huNumberPt` / `srNoText` — so both renderers break lines identically. `fonts.<role>` now carries `{pt, weight}`; weights (HU/material bold, meta regular, caption bold) and pure `#000` live in the spec. `fits()` now also fails when the HU number can't fit at its 6.5 pt floor (reuses the existing skip path — no controller change).
- **Renderers**: `HuLabelPrinter.section` emits one div per shared-`wrap` line (no more `-webkit-line-clamp`) + a 0.065em baseline calibration so HTML/PDF baselines align; `HuLabelPdf.drawLabel` draws the same `wrap` lines and uses `ellipsize` (dropped the jsPDF-only `fitText`/`splitTextToSize`). Size A (captions hidden) prefixes a bare serial with "SR ".
- **Measured before → after (typical data; real PDF font sizes via pdfplumber):**

  | size | HU→MAT→META→CAP pt (before) | HU→MAT→META→CAP pt (after) | HU/MAT | MAT/META | bottom slack mm (before→after) |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | A | 8 / 6 / 6 / – | **9 / 7 / 6 / –** | 1.33→1.29 | **1.00→1.17** | 3.9→1.7 |
  | B | 13 / 9 / 9 / 6 | **14 / 10 / 8 / 6.5** | 1.44→1.40 | **1.00→1.25** | 2.8→3.3 |
  | E | 18 / 11 / 11 / 7 | **20 / 13 / 10 / 7.5** | 1.64→1.54 | **1.00→1.30** | 15.1→5.3 |
  | C | 18 / 11 / 11 / 7 | **26 / 14 / 11 / 8** | 1.64→1.86 | **1.00→1.27** | 15.1→5.4 |
  | D | 32 / 11 / 9 / 7 | **36 / 16 / 12 / 8.5** | 2.91→2.25 | 1.22→**1.33** | 10.8→5.4 |

  Fixes: meta is now a real secondary level everywhere (MAT/META ≥1.15, was 1.00); C ≠ E; D material 11→16 pt; bottom slack evened (C/E were 15 mm). Smallest x-height @203 dpi: A 8.85 dots (meta 6 pt, floor — only on A); B/E/C/D rose to 9.6/11.1/11.8/12.5 dots (captions larger).
- **Verification (measured):** unit invariants (hierarchy ratios, weights, stack gaps ≥0.8 mm, bottom slack ∈[0.5, pad+1.5], wrap/huNumberPt/measure) on all 5 sizes; **0 overflow / 0 placeholders / 1 page / empty→"-"** across 5 sizes × {typical, worst 152-char+20-digit+34-serial, empty}; barcode untouched — `pdfinfo` A 141.7×70.9 / B 283.5×141.7 / **E 283.5×283.5** / C 425.2×283.5 / D 566.9×283.5 pt, `zxing` decode OK on all 5 at **300 and 203 dpi**; print-vs-PDF parity — font sizes + line breaks identical, vertical position ≤0.16 mm on 14/15 role-points (lone outlier: A date 0.365 mm, a Chrome PDF-export rounding artifact on 6 pt text — the material line on the same label is 0.023 mm with identical code). Full `npx jest test/unit` **128 suites / 2229 tests**; `ui5lint` clean; `git diff --check` clean.
- **Report-only flags (bar logic unchanged):** A (3.41) and E (6.54) narrow-bar widths aren't whole dots @203 dpi.
- **Decisions:** leftover space → even gaps (per the instruction), so square E has airy rhythm; A serial prefix "SR "; HU-overflow reuses the barcode-worded `huLabelTooSmall` message.
- Files: `util/HuLabelLayout.js`, `util/HuLabelPrinter.js`, `util/HuLabelPdf.js`, `test/unit/wm/huLabelLayout.test.js`, `test/unit/wm/handlingUnitLabel.test.js`.
- **For physical stock:** confirm the A (50×25, 6 pt meta) and the 3.41/6.54-dot narrow bars scan on the actual thermal printer at 203 dpi; verify the "SR " serial prefix and the D 36 pt HU number read at distance.

### 2026-10-07 — HU list: Plant / Storage Location showed "- / -" — root-caused to blank monitor header, fixed by item enrichment

- **Symptom**: in `#/wm/handling-unit` the "Plant / Storage Location" column showed "- / -" for many HUs (notably recently created ones).
- **Debug (live, read-only, AGENTS.md discovery — SAP reachable at the dump host, app creds from `.env.local`, values never printed)**:
  1. Ruled out the UI: reproduced in a backend-less `ui5 serve` with a stub — the cell renders `1120 / CS01` when the row has data, `- / -` only when `Plant`/`StorageLocation` are empty. UI binding, the CDS `HandlingUnitListItem` type (declares both fields), and the adapter mapping are all correct.
  2. `$metadata` of `C_HANDLINGUNITMONITOR_CDS`: entity `HandlingUnitType` **does** expose `Plant`/`PlantName`/`StorageLocation`/`StorageLocationName` (so `$select` is valid; the list read never failed).
  3. Live sample reads: the monitor **header** returns `Plant`/`StorageLocation` **blank** for many HUs (0/15 in default order; 0/15 among the most-recent HUs) — but a header `$filter=Plant eq '1120'` returns 253 rows *with* Plant/SLoc set, i.e. the header is populated only for HUs assigned at header level. The real plant/sloc for the blank ones live on the HU **item**: `I_HandlingUnitItemCombined` for HU 2000020330 → `Plant 1120 / SLoc CS02`.
  - **Root cause**: `list()` read only the monitor header, so HUs without header-level plant/sloc rendered "- / -".
- **Fix** (`srv/integration/s4hana/wm/HandlingUnitAdapter.js`): after the header read, for rows whose `Plant` or `StorageLocation` is blank, enrich from the first HU item (lowest `HandlingUnitItem`) via the same `I_HandlingUnitItemCombined` view `labels()` already uses — new `_firstItemPlantSloc(ids)` (OR-batched by `LABEL_HU_CHUNK`=50, best-effort: a chunk read failure leaves "-", list still renders) + `LIST_ITEM_PLANT_SELECT`. Header-populated values are left untouched; the extra read runs only when something is blank (so a plant-scoped user, whose rows all passed the header `Plant eq` filter, incurs no extra read).
- **Validation**:
  - **End-to-end live**: ran the real `HandlingUnitAdapter.list({})` against SAP — **962/1000 rows now have Plant/SLoc** (was blank for the recent ones); the first 10 recent HUs now show `1120 / CS02`, `1130 / FG01`, … The ~38 still blank are HUs with no items (empty packaging HUs) — correctly "-".
  - Unit: 2 new adapter tests (blank header filled from the first item incl. lowest-item ordering; no item read when the header already has both). `npx jest test/unit` **128 suites / 2216 tests pass**; `npx cds compile srv` OK; `eslint` clean; `git diff --check` clean.
- **Known follow-up (not in this fix)**: a plant-scoped (non-Admin) user still won't *see* an HU whose monitor **header** plant is blank even if its **item** plant is their plant, because `list()` filters on the header `Plant` field. Making plant scoping item-aware is a separate authorization change (and the enrichment above deliberately does not widen what rows are returned). Noted for a later decision.
- Files: `srv/integration/s4hana/wm/HandlingUnitAdapter.js`, `test/unit/wm/handlingUnitAdapter.test.js`.

### 2026-10-07 — HU labels: selectable stock size (A/B/C/D) for every Print/Download, one shared size-aware layout

- **What**: Every HU Print/Download action (`onPrintAll` / `onPrintSelected` / `onPrintCurrentPage` / `onDownloadSelected`, per-row `onRowPrint` / `onRowDownload`, and the detail page `onPrint` — 7 call sites) now opens a **Select label size** dialog first. Four stock sizes replace the old fixed 4×4in label: **A Small 50×25mm** (instruments/sensors, no captions), **B Medium 100×50mm** (default), **C Large 150×100mm**, **D Heavy Duty 200×100mm** (32pt distance HU number). The chosen size feeds **one geometry spec** consumed identically by the HTML print builder and the jsPDF renderer, so print and PDF match. Last choice remembered per browser (`localStorage["saps4hana_fiori_hu_label_size"]`, default B). Cancel aborts with no read/print/download. Existing pipeline reused unchanged (chunked `labels()`, `prepare`, fallback toast, plant scoping, `/printing` + row-busy double-click guards). No new dependencies; jsPDF stays lazy-loaded.
- **Scannability gate**: after `prepare`, each label's barcode narrow-bar width (`innerW / Code128 modules`) is checked against the size minimum (A/B 0.25mm, C 0.33mm, D 0.40mm). An HU too dense for the chosen size is moved to the skipped list naming the next-larger size (`huLabelTooSmall`), surfaced via the existing job-summary/toast path; a single too-dense row toasts and prints nothing. Fonts scale from label height with a 6pt floor; material clamps to 1/2/3/2 lines by size; filenames `HU_<hu>_<A|B|C|D>.pdf` (row) and `HU_labels_<yyyymmdd_hhmm>_<A|B|C|D>.pdf` (bulk). The old `_confirmCount` MessageBox was merged into the dialog (bulk count on the confirm button + a `>500` warning MessageStrip).
- **Files**: new `util/HuLabelLayout.js` (sizes + geometry + `fits()`, single source of truth), `util/HuLabelSizeDialog.js` (shared picker, localStorage, used by both controllers), `view/SelectLabelSizeDialog.fragment.xml` (SingleSelect list with name/mm/description + proportional preview rect); modified `util/Barcode.js` (`toSvg(text,wMm,hMm)` optional mm dims; `bars()`/`encode()` untouched), `util/HuLabelPrinter.js` + `util/HuLabelPdf.js` (size-aware from the shared spec; removed the hard-coded 4×4/288pt constants; jsPDF `orientation` set explicitly so landscape stock sizes don't swap W/H), `controller/HandlingUnits.controller.js` + `controller/HandlingUnitDetail.controller.js` (open the dialog, thread the size key, fit-check), `css/style.css` (`.huSizePreview`), `i18n/i18n.properties` + `i18n_en.properties` (18 keys each, in sync).
- **Validation**:
  - Unit: new `test/unit/wm/huLabelLayout.test.js` (geometry table, mm→pt, 6pt floor, `fits` 20-digit→A ok / 20-alnum→A fails→B); updated `handlingUnitLabel.test.js` (@page mm, per-size PDF page dims 50×25/100×50/150×100/200×100mm, default B) and `handlingUnitsController.test.js` (size pass-through + `_<size>` filename suffix, Cancel→no read/download, `_partitionByFit`). **Full `npx jest test/unit` = 128 suites / 2212 tests pass.**
  - `ui5lint` clean; `test/unit/controller/uiConsistency.test.js` 7/7 (both i18n bundles in sync); `git diff --check` clean.
  - PDF page sizes verified earlier this session via headless Chrome + `pdfinfo`: A 141.73×70.87, B 283.47×141.73, C 425.20×283.47, D 566.93×283.47 pt (all 3 pages, ±tolerance); `zxing-cpp` decoded HU 2000019997 → PASS on A/B/C/D; A and D labels rendered to PNG and visually confirmed (ellipsis clamp on A, captions + big HU number on D).
  - **Dialog verified live in a backend-less `ui5 serve`** (login/SAP not required to render the fragment): all 4 size cards render with name/mm/description + proportional preview, B preselected, i18n text correct in both the title and rows, clicking Large updates the selected key to C, Cancel closes with no side effect. Screenshot captured.
