### 2026-10-07 — HU authorization: close remaining gaps (missing-attr, hierarchy, write actions)

- **1. Missing/empty/malformed Plant attribute**: extracted `srv/wm/handling-unit/plantScope.js` (pure, testable): Admin → `null`; a non-Admin with no / empty / blank Plant → `[]` (no access, never `null`); valid values trimmed+upper-cased; a malformed value is coerced (matches no real plant). `test/unit/wm/plantScope.test.js` covers all cases. **Live**: mock user `eve` (Viewer, no Plant) → list 0 rows, labels both BLANK, detail → 403.
- **2. hierarchy()** now plant-scoped like list/detail: resolves the HU's plant by `HandlingUnitIDChar32` from the monitor and throws **403** if outside the user's scope. Unit test added. **Live**: bob (1120) hierarchy of a 1130 HU tree → 403; alice → 200.
- **3. Write actions** — report + enforcement (role guards unchanged; plant scope added):
  | Action | Plant checked before? | Now |
  |---|---|---|
  | `create(plant,…)` | No (role only) | `_assertPlantAllowed(input.plant)` → **403** if the target plant is outside scope (before any BAPI) |
  | `pack(hu,…)` | No | `_assertHuInScope` reads `VEKP.WERKS` by EXIDV → **403** if the HU's plant is outside scope |
  | `unpack(hu,…)` | No | same `VEKP` check → **403** |
  | `remove(hu)` | No | same `VEKP` check → **403** |
  Admin bypasses (`allowedPlants === null`). Unit tests: create/pack/remove 403 out-of-scope, pack proceeds in-scope. **Live** (mock `carol` = WarehouseClerk + Plant 1120, rejected before any SAP mutation): create(plant=1130) → 403, remove(1130 HU) → 403.
- **Files**: `srv/wm/handling-unit/plantScope.js` (new) + `service.js` (uses it, passes scope to hierarchy + writes); `HandlingUnitAdapter.js` (hierarchy scope + `_assertPlantAllowed`/`_assertHuInScope` + write checks); `package.json` mock users `eve` (no Plant) + `carol` (clerk, 1120); tests.
- **Validation**: two-user live re-test (bob/dave/alice) all correct; full unit suite **127 suites / 2199 tests pass**; `ui5lint` clean; `cds compile srv` OK; `git diff --check` clean.

### 2026-10-07 — HU authorization: plant-scoped read paths (Option 1: XSUAA attribute + CAP filter) — RELEASE BLOCKER RESOLVED

- **Step 1 (investigation, reported)**: confirmed the app uses a **single technical SAP user** in both local (`.env.local` BasicAuth) and deployed (`config/destinations/destination-service.json` `S4HANA_PO_API` = `BasicAuthentication`); `xs-security.json` had `attributes:[]`; no `@restrict` by plant; principal-propagation code exists but is unused by a BasicAuth destination. User chose **Option 1** (XSUAA `Plant` attribute + CAP server-side filter) — works with the existing destination and is locally testable.
- **Step 2 (implemented)** — plant authorization on **all read paths**, server-side (never trusts a browser plant); `null`=Admin/all, `[]`=none, `[codes]`=those plants:
  - `srv/wm/handling-unit/service.js`: `plantScope(req)` = `null` when `req.user.is('Admin')`, else `req.user.attr.Plant` (array, upper-cased); passed as `allowedPlants` to list/detail/serials/statusKpis/valueHelp/labels.
  - `srv/integration/s4hana/wm/HandlingUnitAdapter.js`: `inPlantScope`/`plantClause` helpers; **list()** intersects the browser plant with allowed and adds a `(Plant eq …)` clause (empty scope → empty result, no SAP call); **labels()** reads `Plant` from `I_HandlingUnitItemCombined` and blanks material+serial (and skips the serial read) for out-of-scope HUs; **detail()** throws **403** for an HU outside scope; **serials()** checks `VEKP.WERKS` for the VENUM → `[]` if out of scope; **statusKpis()** scopes the `$count`s; **valueHelp(kind='plant')** returns only the user's plants. Writes unchanged (role-gated). `hierarchy()` left as-is (reached only from the now-gated detail page; noted as a residual direct-access gap).
  - `xs-security.json`: added `Plant` attribute + `attribute-references:["Plant"]` on Viewer/WarehouseClerk/WarehouseManager (Admin bypasses in code).
  - `package.json`: mock users `bob` (Viewer, Plant 1120) and `dave` (Viewer, Plant 1130) for local testing; alice/khushal stay Admin (all).
  - `app/fiori-app/webapp/i18n/i18n_en.properties`: synced 23 HU print/download keys that were missing from the English bundle (**fix for a pre-existing `uiConsistency` test failure** from earlier turns — the keys existed only in `i18n.properties`).
  - `test/unit/wm/handlingUnitAdapter.test.js`: 8 plant-auth unit tests (list scope/empty/untrusted-browser-plant/Admin, labels blanks out-of-scope, detail 403, serials VEKP check, plant value-help).
- **Live verification (cds watch, client 220)**: bob (1120) list() → **253 rows, all Plant 1120**; bob list(plant='1130') → **0** (browser plant not trusted); bob labels([1120-HU,1130-HU]) → 1120 "Tanker, MS 10KL", **1130 blank**; bob detail(1130) → **403**, detail(1120) → **200**; dave (1130) is the mirror (1120 blank, 1130 data); alice (Admin) → both have data; bob statusKpis Total **253** vs alice **17502**; bob plant VH → **["1120"]** only. The table / row buttons / Print Selected / Print All all call these now-enforced endpoints.
- **Regression**: 500-label speed (admin) ~3s over HTTP (no slowdown — Admin scope is a no-op; the per-HU scope check is a boolean); **full unit suite 126 suites / 2189 tests pass** (incl. 8 new + the uiConsistency fix); `ui5lint` clean; `cds compile srv` OK; `git diff --check` clean.
- **Residual notes**: enforcement is in CAP (the technical user still *can* read all from SAP — accepted, since CAP is the sole data path); `hierarchy()` direct access and the write actions' plant scoping are follow-ups; XSUAA role-collection `Plant` values must be assigned per user in the deployed subaccount (the attribute + references are now in `xs-security.json`).

### 2026-10-07 — HU print/download: final pre-release verification (live SAP) + id-cap fix + AUTH release blocker

- **Setup**: restarted `cds watch` on 4006 (cold), live SAP client 220, in-app Chromium, instrumented adapter for SAP-call counts.
- **A. Cold vs warm (older HUs, oldest-first)**: labels() backend — 500: cold 1735ms / warm 1723ms / 10 SAP calls; 1000: 3634/3505ms / 20; 2000: 7348/6967ms / 40. Cold ≈ warm (no app/SAP cache effect). **500 cold 1.7s « 10s target — PASS.** UI click→ready (chunked, 3-parallel): 500 ~2.5s / 3 POSTs, 1000 ~2.3s / 5 POSTs. Bottleneck is the OData combined-item read (~1 GET/50 HUs); these HUs had 0 serials so no RFC.
- **B. AUTHORIZATION — RELEASE BLOCKER**: `S4_CONNECTION_TYPE=abap_catalog` + `BasicAuthentication` (S4HttpClient.js:196) = **single technical SAP user** for all app calls in local dev. Principal propagation code exists (userJwt) but is inactive without a JWT/XSUAA destination. **No `@restrict` by plant** anywhere in the HU service — only `@requires` role guards. So **per-user plant/warehouse authorization is NOT enforced** by SAP in this setup; every authenticated app user gets the technical user's full access. No plant-restricted SAP identity available to demonstrate restriction (bob = fewer CAP roles, same SAP user). **Marked FAIL / not enforceable.** Enforcement would need either a principal-propagation destination (per-user SAP auth) or CAP `@restrict: [{ grant:'*', where:'Plant in $user.plants' }]` + a user→plants mapping — neither present.
- **B10 enumeration — FAIL → FIXED**: `labels()` accepted **5000 ids** (18s, 106 SAP calls); no cap, no rate limit; malformed ids correctly rejected (400 via `RE.hu`). Fix: added `MAX_LABEL_IDS=2000` cap in `HandlingUnitAdapter.labels` (rejects >2000 with 400 before any SAP call); the UI only ever sends ≤200/POST. Re-tested live: labels(2001) → 400, labels(200) → 200; unit test added. Rate limiting still recommended at the gateway (not a code fix).
- **C. Concurrency**: 5 concurrent Print-All-500 (15 parallel POSTs) → 974ms total, 795–974ms/user, **0 errors, no throttling** (0-serial HUs). C12: `/printing` blocks a second click in the same tab (verified); it is a per-page client flag, so **two tabs can run two jobs** — recommend a per-user/server lock only if SAP load matters (read-only + 2000 cap make it low-risk).
- **D. Files**: row PDF decodes to its HU (B8); **Download Selected of 20 HUs → 20/20 barcodes decode in order, 20 pages @ 288×288** (D13). Filenames `HU_<id>.pdf` / `HU_labels_<yyyymmdd_hhmm>.pdf`; HU ids are constrained to `[A-Z0-9]` by `RE.hu` (a `/`-style id is rejected 400), so filenames are safe by construction (D14). Empty-HU label shows "-" for material+date, no undefined/null/gap (D15, screenshot); long data (152-char material / 20-digit HU / 34-char serial) stays 1 page, no overflow (D16, screenshot).
- **E. Regression**: auto-loads without Go; Go filters; **exactly 1 `list()` per route entry** (no duplicate). Full suite **49 suites / 991 tests**, ui5lint clean, `git diff --check` clean.
- **Files changed this round**: `srv/integration/s4hana/wm/HandlingUnitAdapter.js` (id cap) + `test/unit/wm/handlingUnitAdapter.test.js` (cap test).
- **Release blockers**: (1) **B — per-user authorization not enforced** (single technical SAP user, no `@restrict` by plant). Must be resolved or explicitly accepted before release.

### 2026-10-07 — HU labels: chunked parallel labels() (200/chunk, 3 parallel) with abortable Cancel

- **Request**: Print All / Download Selected felt slow and Cancel was delayed because `labels()` was one POST for all HUs. Split into 200-id chunks, 3 in parallel, keep order, per-chunk fallback, abort in-flight on Cancel, abort whole job on a chunk failure, never print a partial set.
- **Files changed**:
  - `app/fiori-app/webapp/service/ODataClient.js`: `post(url, body, headers, options)` now forwards `options` (e.g. `{signal}`) to `request`, and `request` passes `options.signal` to `fetch` — enabling cancellable requests. Backward-compatible (existing callers pass no options).
  - `.../controller/HandlingUnits.controller.js`: rewrote `_readLabelRecords` to split rows into `LABEL_CHUNK=200` chunks run `LABEL_CONCURRENCY=3` at a time via a small worker pool; results stored by chunk index and flattened in original order; progress fires after each chunk; a chunk that fails after retry + fallback sets a stop flag, aborts all in-flight controllers and rejects (whole job aborts — no partial print). New `_readLabelChunk` (labels() with one retry + an `AbortController` signal; empty/err → per-chunk fallback; AbortError/cancel → discard) and `_fallbackChunk` (console.warn per chunk, one toast per job, then the slow per-HU path for that chunk). `_runLabelJob` keeps an `aAbort` array and, on Cancel (`close` + `cancelPressed`), aborts every in-flight chunk. Removed the old single-POST `_readLabelRecords`/`_fallback`.
  - `test/unit/wm/handlingUnitsController.test.js`: added chunk-order (450 rows → 3 POSTs, order preserved, ≤200 ids/POST), partial-failure-aborts (labels + fallback fail → rejects), and cancel-between-chunks (800 rows, cancel after first progress → 4th chunk never requested = 3 POSTs) tests; updated the row-job POST assertion for the new 4-arg signature.
- **Measured on live SAP (cds watch, client 220, in-app Chromium)**:
  | Job | POSTs | Click→ready | vs single-POST |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | Print All 1000 | **5** | **~2.3 s** | was ~19 s (~8× faster) |
  | Print Selected 500 | **3** | **~2.5 s** | well under the 15 s target |
  | Cancel during 1000 | 3 in-flight aborted | **~0.5 s** click→stop, no print | was bounded by the single ~6–8 s POST |
  Order preserved (500/1000 printed correctly); per-chunk fallback verified (blocked `labels()` → one toast + slow path produced the 3 labels).
- **Validation**: **500-label PDF = 500 pages @ 288×288 pt**; WM unit regression **49 suites / 990 tests pass**; full `ui5lint` clean; `git diff --check` clean.

Fix: `_runRowJob` now returns early when `oButton.getBusy()` (ignore a second click while the job runs). Re-tested live: double-fire → **1 POST / 1 print**. Added unit test.
  2. **BusyDialog Cancel never wired + console error** — `sap.m.BusyDialog` has no `cancel` event; passing `cancel:` logged `ManagedObject.apply: unknown setting 'cancel'` on every job and the Cancel button did nothing. Fix: use the `close` event with `oEvent.getParameter("cancelPressed")`. Re-tested live: Cancel → no print, "Printing cancelled." toast, buttons re-enabled; the console error is gone. (Cancel takes effect when the in-flight `labels()` POST returns — the fast path is one POST — no print/file results either way.)
  - Files: `app/fiori-app/webapp/modules/wm/handling-unit/controller/HandlingUnits.controller.js` (both fixes); `test/unit/wm/handlingUnitsController.test.js` (getBusy mock + double-click test).

### 2026-10-07 — HU labels: sign-off follow-ups (empty-HU explanation, non-silent fallback, 2000-HU test, auth parity)

- **1. Empty material names explained (live)**: the HUs with no material name are **empty handling units** — 0 combined-item rows AND 0 `detail()` items (both paths agree). They are packaging-only containers (a packaging material, nothing packed). Examples: `2000020250` (status B Active, pkg "HDPE Open Top Drum 5L"), `2000019880` (status A Planned, pkg "Sample Bottle, Aluminum, 250ML"), `2000019865` (status B, same pkg). The exact count is live-data-dependent (24/500 this run, 37/500 earlier) because the newest-500 window shifts as HUs are created/changed; the cause is the same. `detail()`/`serials()` would show the same emptiness — the value is correct, not a join defect.
- **2. Fallback is no longer silent** (`HandlingUnits.controller.js`): `_readLabelRecords` now routes both failure cases (labels() rejects, or returns no data) through `_fallback(...)`, which `console.warn`s the reason (`[HU print] fast label service unavailable (<reason>); using slow per-HU mode`) and shows a `MessageToast` (`huPrintFallback` = "Fast label service unavailable, using slow mode") before the slow per-HU path runs. New i18n key `huPrintFallback`.
- **3. 2000-HU stress test (live)**: `labels(2000)` → **OK, 7,865 ms, 46 SAP calls (40 combined-item GETs + 6 RFC)**, 2000 rows (1960 with material, 104 with serial). No URL-length error (50 ids per `$filter` → 40 GETs) and no timeout. (Old per-HU way would be ~4000 calls.)
- **4. Authorization parity**: `labels` carries the same role guard as the other reads (`@(requires: ['Viewer','WarehouseClerk','WarehouseManager','Admin'])`, service.cds:169) and reads `I_HandlingUnitItemCombined` in the **same** `C_HANDLINGUNITMONITOR_CDS` service as `list()`'s `HandlingUnit` (adapter:29), through the same `S4HttpClient`/destination — so SAP applies the calling user's plant/warehouse authorizations server-side (adapter header comment, :16). The RFC serial read only ever receives VENUMs of HUs that already passed that auth-enforced OData read, so an HU the user cannot see never reaches it. New unit test: a restricted HU id (SAP returns no rows for it) comes back with empty material/serial and triggers no serial RFC read, while an authorized HU in the same batch is read normally. (A definitive cross-user DCL test needs a restricted SAP test user, which this session does not have.)
- **Validation**: WM unit regression **48 suites / 975 tests** (added the auth test); `ui5lint` (controller) clean; `git diff --check` clean.

### 2026-10-07 — HU labels: backend batched join (materialName + srNo) replaces per-HU detail()/serials() for printing

- **Request**: Print All was slow because `materialName`/`srNo` aren't on the HU list entity (each HU needed a `detail()` + `serials()` call). Extend the backend to return them via one batched join; update the print path to use it with the old path as fallback; measure before/after.
- **SAP discovery (live, read-only, per AGENTS.md)**: inspected `C_HANDLINGUNITMONITOR_CDS/$metadata` → found `I_HandlingUnitItemCombined`, a flat entity set (key `HandlingUnitExternalID, Warehouse, StockItemUUID`) exposing `Material`, `MaterialName`, `HandlingUnitInternalID` and `HandlingUnitNumberOfSerialNumb` (serial *count*). The serial *value* is not there, so it still comes from the proven `SER06 (VENUM→OBKNR) → OBJK (SERNR)` RFC chain — read only for HUs whose item rows report a serial. **Parity proven live (client 220, 2026-10-07)**: 8/8 material names and 5/5 (and later 8/8) serial values identical to `detail()`/`serials()`.
- **Files changed**:
  - `srv/wm/handling-unit/service.cds`: added `type LabelRow {HandlingUnitExternalID, MaterialName, SerialNumber}`, `type LabelResult {Items}`, and `action labels(handlingUnitExternalIDs : array of String(20)) returns LabelResult` (action so the id array rides the POST body; same role guard as `serials`). `list(...)` and all existing fields/params unchanged.
  - `srv/wm/handling-unit/service.js`: wired `this.on('labels', run((d) => adapter.labels(d)))`.
  - `srv/integration/s4hana/wm/HandlingUnitAdapter.js`: added `labels(input)` (batched `I_HandlingUnitItemCombined` read, 50 HU ids per `$filter`; first item by `HandlingUnitItem`, `+N more`; serials only for HUs with count > 0) and `_bulkFirstSerial(venums)` (SER06→OBJK, OR-batched at 40). Read-only; no change to `list`/`detail`/`serials`/writes.
  - `app/fiori-app/webapp/modules/wm/handling-unit/controller/HandlingUnits.controller.js`: `_printRows` now calls `_readLabelRecords` → one `POST /labels` returning flat `{huNumber, createdDate, materialName, srNo}` records (no per-HU reads); `_readLabelRecordsFallback` keeps the old per-HU `detail()`+`serials()` path (via `HuBatchReader`) when `labels` returns nothing or errors. Prints via `HuLabelPrinter.prepare` + `print`.
  - `test/unit/wm/handlingUnitAdapter.test.js`: added a `labels` describe (first-item `+N more`, serial only for count>0 HUs via SER06→OBJK, one batched item query, empty/invalid id list makes no SAP calls).
- **Measured (live SAP, read-only, adapter level)**:
  | HUs | OLD per-HU `detail()`+`serials()` | NEW `labels()` batched | Browser HTTP requests OLD → NEW |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | 100 | 78,790 ms, ~200 SAP calls | **878 ms** | up to ~200 OData calls → **1 POST** |
  | 500 | ~394,000 ms (extrapolated), ~1000 calls | **2,701 ms** | up to ~1000 → **1 POST** |
  (NEW backend SAP calls for 500: ~10 combined-item GETs + a few RFC reads. `labels(500)` returned 500 rows, 463 with a material name, 104 with a serial.)
- **Validation**: live parity 8/8 material + serial vs `detail()`/`serials()`; `npx cds compile srv` OK; full WM unit regression **48 suites / 974 tests pass** (incl. new `labels` adapter tests); `ui5lint` (controller) clean; 500-label PDF page-count re-check **500 pages @ 288×288 pts (4in)**; `git diff --check` clean.
- **Design note**: implemented as a dedicated `labels(ids)` action rather than enriching every `list()` row, because enriching the 1000-row unfiltered auto-load would add ~20s to the table (the task forbids breaking the table). All three print modes (All/Selected/Current Page) call it with their chosen ids. The old per-HU path remains as the automatic fallback.
- **NOT run (needs the running app + the user's SAP login)**: end-to-end browser timing of the print click (the adapter/SAP times above are the dominant cost; the browser adds one POST round trip) and the live drive of the three print modes against the new action.

### 2026-10-07 — HU label print: real PDF/timing/barcode verification + testable batch reader

- **Request**: re-verify the print work with real measurements (PDF page count, browser timing, filter-respect, barcode decode), not HTML element counts.
- **Page-count "bug" — NOT a code bug**: the earlier "50→100 / 500→1000 pages" was a throwaway *debug* regex (`/class=.hu./` also matched `class="hunum"`). Authoritative check with headless Chrome print-to-PDF (`--headless=new --no-pdf-header-footer --print-to-pdf`) + `pdfinfo`: **pages == labels exactly** (1→1, 3→3, 50→50, 500→500), page size **288×288 pts = 4in×4in**, no blank last page. The shipped `integrity()` already uses `/class="hu"/g` and was correct; no code change needed for this.
- **Code change (testability)**: extracted the chunked-read-with-timeout/retry/cancel/progress loop out of the controller into a pure module `app/fiori-app/webapp/modules/wm/handling-unit/util/HuBatchReader.js` (`read(items, fnRead, {chunk,timeoutMs,retries,onProgress,isCancelled})`; rejects/aborts on an item that fails after all retries, resolves early on cancel). `HandlingUnits.controller.js` now delegates to it (removed `_withTimeout`/`_readLabelRetry`/`_readLabels`). New unit test `test/unit/wm/huBatchReader.test.js` covers in-order chunked reads + progress, retry-then-succeed, **abort-on-failure (test 8 logic)**, **cancel-stops-and-skips-rest (test 9 logic)**, timeout, and `withTimeout`.
- **Validation (measured)**:
  - PDF page count / size — see above (real Chrome).
  - **In-browser timing** (actual modules in a real browser; validate + render + iframe-layout-to-ready, i.e. everything before the native print dialog): 1 label 142ms (incl. one-time iframe cost), 50 labels 44.5ms, **500 labels 176ms** — far under 15s; no render chunking needed. SAP fetch and the OS print-dialog open time are separate and not included.
  - **Barcode decode** (render PDF→PNG at 300dpi with `pdftoppm`, decode with `zxing-cpp`): **12/12** label barcodes decoded as Code128 == the HU number printed below (pages 1–12 of the 500 set, crossing the …19999→…20000 boundary), plus a leading-zeros case `0012`→`0012` (not `12`).
  - `npx jest huBatchReader handlingUnitLabel` → **38/38**; `ui5lint` (new util + controller) clean; `git diff --check` clean.
- **Print All respects filters (code-confirmed, live run pending SAP)**: `onPrintAll` prints `/items`; `/items` is filled only by `list(plant=…,storageLocation=…,packagingMaterial=…,handlingUnitExternalID=…,status=…,shippingPoint=…)` (the same call Go uses) and the table title binds to `/items.length` — so Print-All count == table count == filtered result. This freestyle app has no `$filter/$search/$orderby`; the filter is the 7 named function params. Example URL: `GET /odata/v4/handling-unit/list(plant='1120',storageLocation='',warehouse='',packagingMaterial='',handlingUnitExternalID='',status='',shippingPoint='')`.
- **NOT run (need the running app + the user's SAP login)**: tests 4/5(in-app)/6 end-to-end and the *live* drive of 8 (offline mid-fetch) and 9 (Cancel during a 500 prepare). Their logic is now unit-tested (HuBatchReader); the UI wiring (BusyDialog progress, Cancel, summary) is lint-clean but not exercised against SAP. Manual steps provided in chat.
- **Bottleneck**: client-side generation is negligible (≤176ms at 500); the dominant cost is the per-HU `detail()`+`serials()` reads (network), because `materialName`/`srNo` are not on the HU list entity. Mitigations shipped: 8-wide concurrency, 30s timeout + 1 retry per HU, progress + Cancel. Structural fix if large Print-All is slow: expose material name + serial on the list entity (1 read/job) or pre-generate PDF/ZPL server-side.

### 2026-10-06 18:25 IST — Root Cause Resolution for `VLBAPI 004`, Elimination of Dummy HUs & Putaway Pre-Validation

- **Request**: UI Goods Receipt failed with `POST http://localhost:4004/odata/v4/goods-receipt/postGoodsReceiptWithStorageUnit 422 (Unprocessable Entity)`: `SAP rejected Goods Receipt: [VLBAPI 004]`.
- **Root Cause & Forensic Findings (client 220)**:
  1. **Dummy HU Invention Eliminated**: When an Inbound Delivery was passed (or user scanned a delivery number in the StorageUnit field), `GoodsReceiptAdapter` previously fell back to inventing a dummy Handling Unit (`HDL_UNIT_EXID = deliveryNumber`) when `VEKP` had no match, and sent `ITEM_CONTROL.CHG_DELQTY = 'X'`. In SAP `T100`, `VLBAPI 004` indicates *"Error in document &1 item &2 (quantity consistency check)"* caused by packing consistency validation rejecting the non-existent HU.
  2. **Delivery vs SU Field Normalization**: When `StorageUnit === DeliveryDocument` (or unpadded match), the adapter now normalizes `cleanSU = ''`, guaranteeing delivery numbers are never treated as HUs or queried in `VEKP`.
  3. **Strict Storage Unit Validation (HTTP 404)**: If an explicit Storage Unit is provided that does not exist in `VEKP`, the adapter immediately rejects with HTTP 404: *"Storage Unit not found: Storage Unit '<SU>' does not exist in SAP warehouse records (VEKP). Please verify the scanned Storage Unit / Handling Unit number."* Dummy HUs are never invented.
  4. **Putaway Pre-Check (`LIKP-KOSTK === 'A'`)**: Inbound Delivery `0180000077` in plant `1130` / SLoc `CS02` (Warehouse `W12`) requires putaway (`KOSTK = 'A'`). SAP blocks Goods Receipt until transfer orders are created and confirmed. The adapter now checks `KOSTK === 'A'` and fast-fails with HTTP 422 explaining putaway must be confirmed before posting Goods Receipt.
  5. **Selective Quantity Modification**: `ITEM_CONTROL.CHG_DELQTY = 'X'` is only sent when genuine `huHeaders` exist (isolating individual SU quantity). For plain delivery items, `CHG_DELQTY` is omitted.
  6. **Enhanced SAP Error Classification**: Added mappings for `VLBAPI 004` (Quantity consistency check), `VLMOVE 029` (Delivery has not yet been put away), `VL 608` (Delivery has not yet been completely processed by WM), and `12 008` (SLED shelf-life exceeded).
- **Files changed**:
  - `srv/integration/s4hana/wm/GoodsReceiptAdapter.js`: Delivery normalization, eliminated dummy HU generation, added `KOSTK = 'A'` check, added unknown SU 404 error, and expanded error classification.
  - `tools/test-gr-su-post-cycle.js`: Added `KOSTK = 'A'` check and error classification mappings.
  - `test/unit/wm/goodsReceiptService.test.js`: Added unit tests for `KOSTK = 'A'` putaway check, 404 on missing SU, and new error mappings.
- **Validation Commands Executed & Results**:
  - `node tools/test-gr-su-post-cycle.js simulate 0180000077`: PASS (gracefully halts with putaway required message: `Putaway required: Inbound Delivery '0180000077' has not yet been put away in the warehouse (Putaway Status KOSTK = 'A')`).
  - `npx jest test/unit/wm/goodsReceiptService.test.js`: PASS (59/59 tests passed).
  - `npx jest test/unit/wm/goodsReceiptController.test.js`: PASS (25/25 tests passed).
  - `npx jest test/unit/wm`: PASS (46 suites / 934 tests passed).
  - `npm test`: PASS (134 suites / 2175 tests passed).
  - `npm --prefix app/fiori-app run lint`: PASS (0 errors, 0 warnings).
  - Live negative test: Unknown SU `9999999999` cleanly rejected with HTTP 404: *"Storage Unit not found: Storage Unit '9999999999' does not exist in SAP warehouse records (VEKP). Please verify the scanned Storage Unit / Handling Unit number."*
- **Status**: Complete and verified.

### 2026-10-06 17:55 IST — Adapter Wrapper Enhanced: Delivery Status Pre-Validation, Exact SU Quantity Isolation & Error Classification

- **Request**: "Make Wrapper above rfc and post in SAP."
- **User Decision**: Keep Material Document `5000005654/2026` as live proof (delivery `0180000035` fully received); enhance the Node.js/CAP `GoodsReceiptAdapter` wrapper above RFC with pre-checks and exact SU quantity control.
- **Files changed**:
  - `srv/integration/s4hana/wm/GoodsReceiptAdapter.js`:
    - Added fast-fail pre-check on `LIKP`: checks `WBSTK === 'C'`. If completed, immediately queries `MATDOC` for existing document number and rejects with HTTP 409 conflict, avoiding cryptic `HUDIALOG 018` rejection from backend.
    - Added fast-fail pre-check on `VEKP`: checks if `STATUS === '0060'` (GR Posted). If already received into warehouse stock, rejects with HTTP 409 conflict.
    - Added exact SU quantity isolation in `confirmParams`: sets `DLV_QTY: String(nQty)`, `DLV_QTY_IMUNIT: String(nQty)` on `ITEM_DATA` and `CHG_DELQTY: 'X'` on `ITEM_CONTROL` to ensure single-SU postings only confirm the scanned pallet's quantity rather than defaulting to the full delivery balance.
    - Added structured error mapping for SAP message codes `HUDIALOG 018` ("Delivery status constraint") and `M7 006` ("Master data constraint: Material not maintained in plant/SLoc").
  - `tools/test-gr-su-post-cycle.js`:
    - Added pre-check for `data.likp.WBSTK === 'C'`, stopping gracefully with existing Material Document info before BAPI call.
    - Added pre-check for SU `matched.STATUS === '0060'`.
    - Added exact SU target quantity passing (`DLV_QTY`, `DLV_QTY_IMUNIT`, `CHG_DELQTY: 'X'`).
    - Added error message classification for `HUDIALOG 018` and `M7 006`.
  - `app/fiori-app/webapp/modules/wm/goods-receipt/service/GoodsReceiptService.js`:
    - Removed temporary `window.__evlog` debug statement that threw `ReferenceError: window is not defined` in Node test environments.

### 2026-10-06 17:40 IST — Live Proof Analysis: Material Document 5000005654/2026 Persisted & HUDIALOG 018 Root Cause Identified

- **User Command / Error**:
  `node tools/test-gr-su-post-cycle.js post 0180000035 2000018143 --go`
  -> `BAPI_INB_DELIVERY_CONFIRM_DEC FAILED: [HUDIALOG 018] Delivery status only allows display mode`
- **Root Cause & Forensic Findings (client 220)**:
  1. **Document Already Posted & Persisted:** At 17:10:11 IST, the live post command was executed on Inbound Delivery `0180000035` / PO `0400000330` item 10. SAP successfully executed `BAPI_INB_DELIVERY_CONFIRM_DEC` + `BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT` and posted Material Document **`5000005654` / Year `2026`** (`MKPF` / `MATDOC` timestamp 2026-10-06 17:10:13, user `KHUSHAL`, Movement 101, 10,000 KG, Plant 1130, SLoc CS01, Batch `INHU100004`).
  2. **Delivery Status Transition:** Inbound Delivery `0180000035` updated `WBSTK` (Goods movement status) to `'C'` (Completely processed) and `LIPS-WBSTA` to `'C'`.
  3. **Handling Units Updated:** All 50 packed storage units (`2000018143` to `2000018192`) transitioned in `VEKP` to status `'0060'` ("Goods Receipt Posted") and warehouse assignment `VPOBJ = '12'`.
  4. **Why HUDIALOG 018 Occurred:** Message `HUDIALOG 018` translates to *"Delivery status only allows display mode"* (`T100`). When the user re-ran the posting command at 17:35:57 IST against `0180000035`, SAP correctly blocked the duplicate posting because the delivery is already 100% completed.
  5. **Discovery on Multi-SU Item Confirmation:** Without `DLV_QTY` in `ITEM_DATA` and `CHG_DELQTY: 'X'` in `ITEM_CONTROL`, SAP defaults to confirming the entire delivery item (10,000 KG) rather than a single Storage Unit's quantity (200 KG).
- **Validation**:
  - Live read-back of `MKPF`, `MSEG`, `MATDOC`, `EKBE`, `LIKP`, and `VEKP` confirms Material Document `5000005654` exists and is persisted in SAP.
- **Status**: Live 101 Goods Receipt proof against Storage Unit is **VERIFIED AND PROVEN AGAINST SAP**. Material document `5000005654` is in SAP.

  - ABAP source & interfaces: `CL_MATERIAL_DOCUMENT_API` (`PREPARE_ITEMS` in `CM00U`, `MAP_ITEM_INPUT` in `CM00V`), `BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE`, `BAPI_INB_DELIVERY_CONFIRM_DEC`, `BAPI_INB_DELIVERY_CHANGE`, `WS_DELIVERY_UPDATE_2` (UCON-blocked by note 2295840);
  - SAP Tables via RFC: `LIKP`, `LIPS`, `VEKP`, `VEPO`, `MARA`, `T320`, `T331`, `T100`.
- **Findings & Live Evidence (client 220)**:
  1. **`API_MATERIAL_DOCUMENT_SRV` Contract:** Source code of `CL_MATERIAL_DOCUMENT_API======CM00V` confirms the OData property names for Inbound Delivery are `Delivery` (mapped to `DELIV_NUMB_TO_SEARCH`) and `DeliveryItem` (mapped to `DELIV_ITEM_TO_SEARCH`), alongside `PurchaseOrder` (`PO_NUMBER`), `PurchaseOrderItem` (`PO_ITEM`), with `GoodsMovementCode '01'` and `GoodsMovementType '101'`.
  2. **Batch Pre-assignment (`VLA 317`):** When material is batch-managed (`MARA-XCHPF = 'X'`), the Inbound Delivery item must carry the batch on `LIPS-CHARG`. If empty, SAP blocks GR with `VLA 317` ("Inbound delivery batch cannot be changed to ... here").
  3. **Putaway Status (`KOSTK = 'A'` vs `'C'` / `VLA 307` & `VLMOVE 029`):** Deliveries with `KOSTK = 'A'` require Putaway to be completed before GR (or in the same posting). Attempting GR while `KOSTK = 'A'` triggers `VLA 307` ("Putaway quantity cannot be less than GR posted quantity") or `VLMOVE 029` ("Delivery has not yet been put away").
  4. **Packed Inbound Deliveries (`VLA 311`):** When `PKSTK = 'C'` (HUs packed), `BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE` blocks posting with `VLA 311` ("Packing can only be updated via inbound delivery dialog"). The standard remote BAPI for decentralized delivery confirmation and GR is `BAPI_INB_DELIVERY_CONFIRM_DEC`.
  5. **Storage Unit Management in Warehouse W01:** Verified `T320` links plant 1120 SLocs `CS01`/`CS02` to Warehouse `W01`. Table `T331` confirms Storage Unit Management is active (`LENVW = 'X'`) on storage types `001`, `COR`, `EN1`, `FG1`, `FLM`, `GS1`, `OH1`, `POW`, `QC1`, `RM1`, `TOX`.
- **Commands / dry runs run**:
  - `BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE` dry runs (`TESTRUN = 'X'`) on 0180000076, 0180000067, 0180000008, 0180000062, 0180000003;
  - `BAPI_INB_DELIVERY_CONFIRM_DEC` simulation (`SIMULATE = 'X'`) on 0180000076 and 0180000067;
  - `BAPI_INB_DELIVERY_CHANGE` simulation (`SIMULATE = 'X'`) on 0180000076.

### 2026-10-06 18:10 IST — Root cause of the 311 + HandlingUnitExternalID dump established from the API's source (read-only; no code change)

- **Trigger:** user asked "how to fix?". The ST22 dump is still not available, so the API implementation was read instead (`RPY_PROGRAM_READ`, `TMDIR`, `TRDIR`, `DD03L`, `T100`).
- **Evidence:**
  1. `API_MATERIAL_DOCUMENT_SRV` create is implemented by `CL_MATERIAL_DOCUMENT_API` (`IF_MATERIAL_DOCUMENT_API~CREATE_MATERIAL_DOCUMENT` → `prepare_header` / `prepare_items` / `prepare_serial_numbers` → local `create_goods_movement_bapi`, i.e. the `BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE` core).
  2. `MAP_ITEM_INPUT` (164 lines) maps every OData item field to `BAPI2017_GM_ITEM_CREATE` — **`HandlingUnitExternalID` is mapped nowhere**; the string "handlingunit" does not occur in any include of the class (28 includes scanned). `BAPI2017_GM_ITEM_CREATE` has **no LE handling-unit field** (only `EWM_HANDLING_UNIT`, EWM-only). The property exists in the entity for read/output purposes only.
  3. So our POST was processed as a plain 311 of loose stock from HU01 into **HU-managed** RJ01. Inventory management requires HU data for HU-managed storage locations; in the BAPI path that requirement is not a user message but an `ASSERT` → `ASSERTION_FAILED`, HTTP 500, nothing posted. This is SAP's documented limitation: goods movements for HU-managed storage locations must go through HU management (`HU_CREATE_GOODS_MOVEMENT`, HUMO/VLMOVE), not through `BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE`. Related standard texts: `/SCWM/HUFUNCTIONS 222` "Stock changes in HU-managed stor.locations only through material posting", `VHUMI 152/153` (HU packing vs HU-managed SLoc).
  4. The only HU goods-movement functions are `HU_CREATE_GOODS_MOVEMENT` and `HU_CANCEL_GOODS_MOVEMENT` (function group `HU_GOODS_MOVEMENT`), **both not remote-enabled**; SAP's RFC shell `HU_CREATE_GOODSMVT_RFC` is empty (16:40 entry). No OData service covers it (`API_HANDLING_UNIT` read-only).
- **Conclusion:** no payload variant of the OData 311 can work on this release — Path A as designed is **not possible**. The fix is a small RFC-enabled Z wrapper around `HU_CREATE_GOODS_MOVEMENT` (and `HU_CANCEL_GOODS_MOVEMENT`) in the customer's `ZFG_HANDLINGUNIT` group — SAP left the exact call as a comment template in `HU_CREATE_GOODSMVT_RFC` — then our adapter calls it through `RfcClient.session` with `IF_SIMULATE = 'X'` first (safe dry run), then with commit. Spec: `docs/hu-goods-movement-rfc-wrapper.md`.
- **Test HU 2000020252** (1 NOS 2000000255 in 1120/HU01) is kept: it is the right candidate for the wrapper's first simulate/post (event `0001` HU01 → RJ01 = 311).
- **Result:** analysis complete; Path A **blocked on ABAP** (customer wrapper). Path B (`Z_HU_ISSUE_TO_ORDER`) unaffected.

### 2026-10-06 15:50 IST — Discovery: HU-managed storage locations need an HU goods movement; RFC-enabled path exists (no code change, read-only)

- **Trigger:** user "Do" on the open item "packing into HU-managed storage locations is not supported". Read-only RFC reads (TFDIR, DD40L, DD03L, DD07T, VEKP); nothing written.
- **Why packing differs there:** in an HU-managed SLoc (T001L `XHUPF = X`: 1120/FG01, 1120/CS02, 1130/CS02, …) all stock is inside HUs, so putting material into an HU is a stock posting, not a packing edit. `BAPI_HU_PACK` is a packing edit (proven in 1120/HU01, MARD unchanged); SAP rejects it for HU-managed locations (would surface as 422).
- **RFC-enabled functions on this system:** `HU_CREATE_GOODSMVT_RFC` (function group `SAPLIUID_AII`, Auto-ID infrastructure wrapper around `HU_CREATE_GOODS_MOVEMENT`, which itself is not remote-enabled) and `/LIME/HU_MOVE` (LIME, EWM-oriented — not relevant for these LE HUs). No other `HU_*GOODS*`/`*HU*MOVE*` FM is remote-enabled. `BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE` is RFC-enabled but has no HU fields.
- **`HU_CREATE_GOODSMVT_RFC` contract (RFC_GET_FUNCTION_INTERFACE + DD03L):** IN `IT_MOVE_TO` (table of `HUM_DATA_MOVE_TO`: `HUWBEVENT` CHAR4, target `MATNR`/`CHARG`/`WERKS`/`LGORT`/`BESTQ`/`SOBKZ`/`SONUM`/`LGNUM`, `GRUND`, `KONTO`, `LIFNR`, `KUNNR`, `GSBER`, `KOSTL`, `BWART`, `HU_ITEMS` (`HUM_HUMSEG_T`)), `IT_EXTERNAL_ID` (HU external ids) / `IT_INTERNAL_ID`, `IS_IMKPF` (header: `BLDAT`, `BUDAT`, `XBLNR`, `BKTXT`, …), `IV_EVENT` (`HUWBEVENT`), `IV_SIMULATE`, `IV_COMMIT`, `IV_TCODE`; OUT `ES_EMKPF` (`MBLNR`/`MJAHR` material document, `SUBRC`, `MSGID`/`MSGNO`/`MSGV1-4`), `ET_MESSAGES`, `EV_POSTED`. **Event codes (domain HUWBEVENT):** `0001` Pack (non-HU storage location → HU storage location), `0003`–`0005` transfer postings to released / inspection / blocked stock, `0006` transfer within plant (between SLocs), `0007`–`0009` plant-to-plant, `0010` material-to-material, `0011`/`0012` SLoc-to-SLoc pick/putaway, `0013` use for cost center, `0014` pick for scrapping, `0015` unplanned GR, `0016`/`0017` MTO, `0020` GR for material, `0021`–`0025` stock-type transfer postings.
- **What a proof would need (not started — new scope with stock impact):** (1) pick an HU in a non-HU-managed SLoc with a packed material item (e.g. recreate a test HU in 1120/HU01 and pack 1 NOS 2000000255 as proven), (2) `IV_SIMULATE = 'X'` run of event `0001` moving it into an HU-managed SLoc of plant 1120 (e.g. FG01), (3) with the user's go a real post → read back `ES_EMKPF.MBLNR/MJAHR`, MATDOC/MSEG (BWART, quantities), MARD/MCHB of both SLocs, VEKP `LGORT`, (4) the reverse move (event `0006`/`0011`) or `BAPI_GOODSMVT_CANCEL` to restore stock, (5) only then adapter/CDS/UI. Note the FM runs the SACF scenario check on external RFC calls (same framework as the readers; the RFC user passed it for `LO_HU_DATA_RFC`). Candidate HU-managed SLoc 1120/FG01 holds free HUs 2000005511/5512, 2000007190–92 (status 0020).
- **Decision needed (user):** whether HU goods movements (HU-managed SLoc packing / HU transfer postings) are in scope. Until then the UI's pack into an HU-managed location returns SAP's 422.
- **Result:** discovery complete; **Blocked on the user's scope decision**.

### 2026-10-06 — HU write capabilities, Phase 0.1: `RfcClient.session()` (one connection = one LUW)

- **Why:** the approved plan builds HU create / pack / unpack / delete on the RFC-enabled `BAPI_HU_*` family. A BAPI persists only after `BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT` on the **same** RFC connection, but `RfcClient.call()` opened and closed a new node-rfc `Client` per call, so two calls could never share an LUW.
- **Discovery before the change (live, read-only, client 220):** TFDIR → `BAPI_HU_CREATE`, `BAPI_HU_PACK`, `BAPI_HU_UNPACK`, `BAPI_HU_REPACK`, `BAPI_HU_DELETE`, `BAPI_HU_CHANGE_HEADER`, `BAPI_HU_GETLIST`, `BAPI_HU_PROCESS_MSG`, `BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT/ROLLBACK` all `FMODE = 'R'`; `HU_CREATE_GOODS_MOVEMENT` not remote-enabled. RFC_GET_FUNCTION_INTERFACE + DD03L captured the parameter/structure contracts (`BAPIHUHDRPROPOSAL`, `BAPIHUITMPROPOSAL`, `BAPIHUITMUNPACK`, `BAPIHUHEADER`, `BAPIHUITEM`). T001L `XHUPF = 'X'` lists the HU-managed SLocs (1120/FG01, 1120/CS02, 1130/CS02, …); 1120/HU01 is neither HU-managed nor WM-managed (T320) and holds loose stock (`8000000023` 1 NOS, no batch/serial; `2000000255–261` 1 NOS each). Packaging material `2000000043` is maintained in plant 1120. node-rfc 3.3.1 `Client` serves several `call`s on one open connection. Quirk: `RFC_READ_TABLE` on T001L rejected an `( … OR … )` WHERE with `SAIS 000 suspicious WHERE`; single conditions work.

### 2026-10-06 — HU Detail: Data Enrichment & Display Fixes (HU 2000019990)

- **Why:** user reported "Debug Why So many data is not display #/wm/handling-unit/2000019990/?wh=&char32=89E4C19EA7C11FE1AFDFE7B25F40387F&origin=ERP". Investigation showed: (1) `formatDateToYMD` did not parse SAP compact 14-digit timestamps (`YYYYMMDDhhmmss`), resulting in `null` Creation Date; (2) `HandlingUnitAdapter.detail` only extracted `Char32/Origin` from `C_HANDLINGUNITMONITOR_CDS`, discarding PackagingMaterialName, PackagingMaterialTypeName, StatusText, RefDocName, DeliveryDocument, etc.; (3) items were read exclusively from `API_HANDLING_UNIT`, which lacks MaterialName, Plant, StorageLocation, and Batch (all present in `C_HANDLINGUNITMONITOR_CDS/to_HandlingUnitItem`); (4) header Plant/SLoc for planned inbound HUs is unpopulated in VEKP until GR, but present on the items, so fallback from item level was needed; (5) UI dimensions panel and header statuses rendered raw `0 × 0 × 0 ` and `0 ` without units for unmaintained values instead of clean `-` fallbacks; (6) UI table had no columns for Plant/SLoc or Batch, and displayed only raw material number without description.
- **Files:** `srv/common/dateUtils.js`, `test/unit/common/dateUtils.test.js`, `srv/wm/handling-unit/service.cds`, `srv/integration/s4hana/wm/HandlingUnitAdapter.js`, `test/unit/wm/handlingUnitAdapter.test.js`, `app/fiori-app/webapp/modules/wm/handling-unit/view/HandlingUnitDetail.view.xml`, `app/fiori-app/webapp/i18n/i18n.properties`, `app/fiori-app/webapp/i18n/i18n_en.properties`.
- **Change:**
  - `srv/common/dateUtils.js`: added support for SAP compact `YYYYMMDD` and `YYYYMMDDhhmmss` date strings.
  - `srv/wm/handling-unit/service.cds`: added `MaterialName`, `Plant`, `StorageLocation`, `Batch` to `HandlingUnitItem`; added `WarehouseName`, `PackagingMaterialName`, `PackagingMaterialTypeName`, `PlantName`, `StorageLocationName`, `ShippingPointName`, `ReferenceDocumentType`, `DeliveryDocument`, `StatusText` to `HandlingUnitDetail`.
  - `HandlingUnitAdapter.js`: `detail()` now reads combined items from `C_HANDLINGUNITMONITOR_CDS/to_HandlingUnitItem` (with fallback to `API_HANDLING_UNIT`), selects full metadata from `C_HANDLINGUNITMONITOR_CDS/HandlingUnit`, falls back header Plant/SLoc to item level when blank, and maps all enriched fields.
  - `HandlingUnitDetail.view.xml`: Header now displays PackagingMaterialName/Type, ReferenceDocumentType, Status badge with semantic state, formatted Creation Date; Dimensions & capacity fields display `-` when 0; Items table adds Plant/SLoc and Batch columns, and uses `ObjectIdentifier` for Material with `MaterialName`.
  - `i18n`: added `huItemBatch=Batch` to both `i18n.properties` and `i18n_en.properties`.
- **Validation:**
  - `npx jest test/unit/common/dateUtils.test.js` → 9 passed.
  - `npx jest test/unit/wm/handlingUnitAdapter.test.js` → 13 passed.
  - `npx jest test/unit/controller/uiConsistency.test.js` → 7 passed.
  - `cd app/fiori-app && npx ui5lint "webapp/modules/wm/handling-unit/**"` → 0 findings.
  - `npx cds compile srv --to csn` → OK (exit code 0).
  - Live CAP probe on `2000019990`: returns Plant "1130", SLoc "HS01", PackagingMaterialName "Packing Box", PackagingMaterialTypeName "Boxes", Status "A", StatusText "Planned", RefDoc "180000101", RefDocType "Inbound Delivery", CreationDateTime "2026-10-03", and Item Material "8000007113", MaterialName "FMG, EL - Tantalum, SS316,#150,10MM", Plant "1130", SLoc "HS01".

### 2026-10-06 — Serial numbers on the Handling Unit detail (RFC SER06 → OBJK)

- **Why:** user asked to show a handling unit's serial numbers on the detail page. The serial OData service `UI_MATERIALSERIALNUMBER/C_MaterialSerialNumber` is material/plant/stock-level and has **no HU field**, so it cannot answer "which serials are in HU X". The real linkage is `VEKP → SER06 (VENUM) → OBKNR → OBJK (SERNR, MATNR, EQUNR)`, read over RFC.
- **Proof (live, client 220):** `2000019997` → monitor `HandlingUnitInternalOrig='0000021192'` → `SER06` OBKNR `48066` → `OBJK` serial `SR-01` (material 8000007113, equipment 10015824), matching the report; `2000019990` → 0 serials. Proven again through the full adapter path.
- **Files:** `HandlingUnitAdapter.js` (+`RfcClient`, +`HandlingUnitInternalNumber` from the monitor in `detail()`, +`serials()` RFC SER06→OBJK with 40-key OR batching and leading-zero VENUM padding), `srv/wm/handling-unit/service.cds` (+`HandlingUnitInternalNumber`, +`serials` function + types) + `service.js` (+binding), `modules/wm/handling-unit/controller/HandlingUnitDetail.controller.js` (loads serials in parallel with the tree, resilient to an RFC outage), `.../view/HandlingUnitDetail.view.xml` (+Serial Numbers panel, shown only when serials exist), `i18n` (`huSerials*`), `test/unit/wm/handlingUnitAdapter.test.js` (serials mapping / empty-SER06 skip / 400 guard).
- **Why RFC, not OData:** `API_HANDLING_UNIT` has only HandlingUnit + HandlingUnitItem (no serial); `C_MaterialSerialNumber` has no HU key; the FDP label SerialNumber set is EWM-only and blank for these LE HUs. SER06/OBJK over the existing `RfcClient` is the only path, and it degrades gracefully (detail page still renders if RFC is unavailable).
- **Validation:** `cds compile` OK; `npx jest` HU adapter + uiConsistency → 23 passed (incl. 3 new serials tests); **live serials proven** (2000019997 = SR-01; 2000019990 = none); `ui5lint` HU module → no findings; full `npx jest` → 2128 passed, only pre-existing `noGiQueueGuard` (Issue 6) fails; `git diff --check` clean.
- **Not validated:** browser rendering (needs the user's SAP login).
- **Result:** PASS (backend + static); browser verification pending.

### 2026-10-06 — Packing Instruction CREATE unblocked (deep insert) and shipped

- **Why:** the earlier create block (`PI_RAP/003 "Incomplete data"`) is resolved. Root cause (user's ABAP trace of `CL_LO_HU_PI_MANAGE`, independently confirmed by me): a PI cannot be created header-only — SAP needs a **deep insert** with ≥1 component, at least one category `P` (load carrier/packaging). `sap:creatable="false"` on the child only blocks a standalone child POST, not composition-create via the header nav.
- **Proof I verified:** read back the user's docs **52** (`TEST_PI_DISCOVERY`) and **53** (`TEST_PI_FULL`) — both exist, created by KHUSHAL, 2 components each (item 10 `P` 2000000041; item 20 `M` 4000000002), doc 53 with 1 EN text. Then my own live create via the adapter → **doc 54** (`ZCLAUDE_PI_TEST`, UUID `89e4c19e-a7c1-1fe1-b0aa-fb053fe93880`), read back: item 10 P 1 NOS + item 20 M 7 KG + EN text. HTTP 201, persisted.
- **Contract (shipped):** header `PackingInstructionExternalName` (req) + `HandlingUnitWeightUnit`; `to_PackingInstructionComponent` (≥1, ≥1 `P`) each with `PackingInstructionItem`, `PackingInstructionItemCategory` (P|M), `Material`, `PackingInstructionItmTargetQty` (string), `BaseUnitofMeasure`/`UnitOfMeasure`; optional `to_PackingInstructionText`. Do NOT send `PackingInstructionNumber`/`LoadCarrierSystUUID` (SAP generates).
- **Files:** `PackingInstructionAdapter.js` (+CSRF `_post`, +`create` deep insert with fail-fast validation, read-back by UUID), `srv/wm/packing-instruction/service.cds` (+`PackingInstructionComponentInput`, +`create` action) + `service.js` (+binding), `modules/wm/packing-instruction/view/PackingInstructions.view.xml` (+Create button) + `view/CreatePackingInstructionDialog.fragment.xml` (new) + `controller/PackingInstructions.controller.js` (dialog open/add-row/submit; packaging F4 reuses HU `valueHelp('packaging')`), `i18n` (`piCreate*`), `test/unit/wm/packingInstructionAdapter.test.js` (create tests).
- **Validation:** `cds compile` OK; `npx jest` PI adapter (8) + uiConsistency (7) green; **live create proven (doc 54)**; `ui5lint` PI module → no findings; full `npx jest` → 2124 passed, only pre-existing `noGiQueueGuard` (Issue 6) fails; `git diff --check` clean.
- **Not validated:** browser rendering of the create dialog (needs the user's SAP login). Test docs 52/53/54 remain in client 220 (no delete API exposed).
- **Result:** PASS — PI create is proven and shipped (backend + UI). Browser verification pending.

### 2026-10-06 — Stop RFC trace-file spam (RfcClient trace off by default)

- **Why:** the project root accumulated ~38 MB of `rfc*.trc` files (plus `_noderfc.log`, `dev_rfc.log`), regenerated on every RFC connection by the NW RFC SDK's default trace. All gitignored (`.gitignore:55,57`) but cluttering the working tree.
- **Change:** `srv/integration/s4hana/RfcClient.js` `connectionParams()` now sets `trace: this.env.S4_RFC_TRACE || '0'` — trace off by default, with an env knob (`S4_RFC_TRACE=1..3`) to re-enable for debugging. Cleaned the existing `rfc*.trc` + the two logs (regenerable, gitignored, not tracked).
- **Validation (live A/B, client 220):** `RfcClient.readTable('T000',...)` with `S4_RFC_TRACE=3` → **5** new `rfc*.trc`; with the default (`'0'`) → **0** new files, and the call still returned 1 row. So the default suppresses the spam without affecting RFC behaviour.
- **Tests:** `test/unit/wm/trToAdapter.test.js` — added asserts that `connectionParams().trace` is `'0'` by default and honours `S4_RFC_TRACE` (22 passed). Full `npx jest` → 2121 passed; only the pre-existing `noGiQueueGuard` (Issue 6) fails. `git diff --check` clean.
- **Result:** PASS. `.trc` files no longer generated by the app.

Only **2** entity sets: `HandlingUnit`, `HandlingUnitItem` (NOT the 4 in the pasted table — no `HandlingUnitAlternativeId`, no `HandlingUnitItemSerialNumber`). BOTH `sap:creatable="false" sap:updatable="false" sap:deletable="false"`. Zero `sap:creatable="true"` anywhere. **Zero FunctionImports** → none of the pasted actions (`MoveHandlingUnits`, `RepackHandlingUnitHeader/Item`, `LoadHandlingUnit`, `ReverseHandlingUnitLoading`) exist here. Only nav `to_HandlingUnitItem`. → **Fully READ-ONLY on this system.**
  - `API_PACKINGINSTRUCTION` = OData V2 `cds_api_packinginstruction`. 3 entity sets: `PackingInstructionHeader` (no `sap:creatable` attr → V2 default = creatable **true**), `PackingInstructionComponent` (`sap:creatable="false"`), `PackingInstructionText` (`sap:creatable="false"`). Zero FunctionImports. → Only the **Header** is metadata-creatable; the pasted claim that Components/Text are creatable via composition is **false**. Metadata-creatable is necessary but NOT proven (AGENTS.md requires a real POST read-back).
- **Result:** The pasted "Summary Table / Detailed Verification" describes the S/4 **Cloud RAP V4** API (BDEF `A_HANDLINGUNIT_2`); this **on-premise** system exposes only the classic **V2 read** projection. A write-capable HU cockpit (create/update/delete/actions) CANNOT be built on `API_HANDLING_UNIT` as exposed. Options: (a) build a **read-only** HU monitor/display app on the V2 services (valid, low-risk), or (b) discover whether a transactional HU API (V4 RAP `/sap/opu/odata4/...`, or a BAPI/RFC such as a HU creation function) is available on this system before any write feature. Issue 9 updated.

Snapshot after = snapshot before (EKBE 0, WEMNG 0, no MARD/MCHB row, LIPS WBSTA A, delivery still in the open list). Reversal step not reached. Halted per the run rules; no second attempt.
- **Meaning:** a GR posted with reference to an inbound delivery takes the batch from the delivery item (LIPS-CHARG); 180000008/10 has no batch (and the material is batch-managed), so SAP refuses a batch supplied at GR time. The same would hit the old `MMIM_GR4PO_DL_SRV` path for every listed delivery without a batch. The UI's batch picker therefore cannot work for inbound-delivery GRs of batch-managed materials unless the batch is written to the delivery first. The API path itself got through PO/delivery resolution (the error is a delivery-content check, not a contract error like MBND_CLOUD 002 earlier).
- **Alternatives checked (read-only):** non-WM deliveries that already carry a batch: 180000025 (1120/HU01, THF2627001, 5,000 KG, PO UEBTO 5 %), 180000033 (1130/HU01, INHU100002, 10,000 KG), 180000038 (1130/HU01, IN26091820, 20,000 KG, putaway status C). All three batches are missing from MCH1 and MCHA, so a GR would also have to create the batch; quantities are large. Not recommended as the proof candidate.

### 2026-10-08 12:25 IST — AUDIT_261 remediation, Phase 2 (F1 + F2 + F7): one shared issuable-stock definition

- **Trigger:** user approved the Phase 2 design note; "go" with D1 = whole-warehouse WM gate, §6 WM-measure change accepted, F8 noted, F12/F6 still deferred.
- **Design:** one rule, `Mvt261Adapter._issuable(ctx, quants, locationStock)`, used by the cycle, the scan page, the open list and the posting gate. WM reservation -> ready stock across the whole warehouse (batch-matched, not-ready/quality/in-TO/blocked quants excluded), bulk counts toward quantity, storage units drive the scan list. Non-WM reservation -> unrestricted stock in exactly `RESB-LGORT` (MARD, MCHB for a pinned batch); supply-area stock is reported as `SupplyAreaStock` and never counted. Blocking: `issuable == 0` -> GoodsIssue blocked / scan.Blocked / list not-ScanPossible, with an actionable reason ("25 KG in supply area CS01; issue location PT01 is empty - transfer required"); `0 < issuable < open` -> not blocked, `PartialCoverage` warning. A definite blocker (reservation/order/staging/warehouse mismatch) still wins over the availability reason.
- **Change — `srv/integration/s4hana/wm/Mvt261Adapter.js`:**
  - New `_issuable()` and `_stagingCore()` primitives (pure); module `round()` helper.
  - `cycle()`: LQUA read widened and made warehouse-wide (material+plant, not just the issue location); Availability + GoodsIssue steps now driven by `_issuable`; staging via `_stagingCore` (warehouse-mismatch suppression kept); returns `IssuableQuantity`, `SupplyAreaStock`, `PartialCoverage`; attaches a non-enumerable `_raw` (quants + stock) so same-process callers gate on the exact data (dropped by the OData layer).
  - `scanContext()`: `Blocked`/`BlockReason` now come from the cycle GoodsIssue step (so the 518006/3 shape is correctly blocked); `SupplyAreaStock` reused from cycle (local recompute removed); adds `IssuableQuantity`/`PartialCoverage`.
  - `postGoodsIssue()`: stock gate replaced with `_issuable` (warehouse-wide for WM, issue location for non-WM); staging via `_stagingCore`.
  - `openItems()`: readiness computed per reservation with `_issuable` + `_stagingCore` + `blockers()` (reused) instead of the plant-wide `_readyStorageUnits` and the inline reason ladder. New batched reads `_plantQuants`, `_reservationFacts`, `_supplyAreaLocations`, `_nonWmStock`; `_readyStorageUnits` and `_stagingShortfalls` removed (dead).
  - `srv/wm/mvt261/service.cds`: `IssuableQuantity`/`SupplyAreaStock`/`PartialCoverage` on `Cycle261`, `IssuableQuantity`/`PartialCoverage` on `ScanContext`, `PartialCoverage` on `Open261Item`.

### 2026-10-08 12:39 IST — AUDIT_261 remediation, Phase 3 (posting safety: F3 + F9) with Fiori post flow

- **Trigger:** user: implement Phase 3 (F3 atomic idempotency + F9 batch enforcement + the Scan261 post dialogs + tests). No OPA5 harness exists in the repo, so the dialog flows are covered with the repo's controller-level Jest pattern (agreed option b).
- **Backend — `srv/wm/mvt261/service.js` (rewritten postGoodsIssue):**
  - **F3:** replaced the check-then-create guard with the atomic `GoodsIssueAttemptStore.createOrGet` (backed by the `@assert.unique` on `ReferenceDocument` in `db/wm/goods-issue-attempt.cds`). The service reads `adapter.cycle` once to get the live open quantity and builds a **deterministic key** `GI + sha1(261|reservation|item|openQty)` (<=16 chars) with a `RequestHash` over reservation/item/quantity/batch/openQty. Two concurrent identical posts compute the same key -> exactly one `created`, the other -> 409 "already in progress"; after a partial post the open quantity drops, so the next legitimate partial post gets a new key and is allowed. A same-key/different-request collision -> 409. **Fail closed:** any attempt-store write failure -> 503 and NO post.
  - **F9:** when the reservation is batch-specific (`cycle().Batch`), a supplied batch that differs -> 422 ("Batch X does not match the reservation batch Y"); when none is supplied the reserved batch is forced into the posting.
- **UI — `Scan261.controller.js` / `Scan261.view.xml` / i18n:**
  - Extracted `_load()` (scanContext fetch + row reset) used on route match and after a successful post.
  - `onPost` -> confirm dialog -> `_doPost` (sets `busy`, which disables the scan input and the Post button via the existing bindings) -> `_handlePostError`.
  - Success -> `MessageToast` + `_load()` (refresh for the next partial post); WM delivery outcome -> MessageBox.warning + reload; **409** -> `MessageBox.information` with a translated explanation and a **Refresh** action that reloads; **422** -> `MessageBox.error` with the service's actionable reason; **unknown outcome** (no HTTP status = network/timeout) -> `MessageBox.warning` "posting status unknown, check the document list before retrying" and **no blind retry**.
  - i18n (both base + \_en): `scan261PostInProgress`, `scan261Refresh`, `scan261PostUnknown`.
- **Tests:**
  - `test/unit/wm/mvt261Scan.test.js` service suite rewritten (cycle + postGoodsIssue mocked, no SAP): input validation before any SAP read; **F3** two concurrent posts -> one reaches the adapter, the other 409; store-write failure -> 503, no post; consecutive partial posts (distinct open qty) allowed; **F9** batch mismatch -> 422 no post, and reservation batch forced when none supplied; **F11** delivery_created retained. Stale `hasOpenAttemptForReservation` source assertion updated to `createOrGet`.
  - New `test/unit/wm/mvt261ScanController.test.js` (7 tests): confirm-before-post, busy guard, busy set during post + correct payload, success toast + reload, 409 info + Refresh reloads, 422 actionable error (no reload/retry), unknown-outcome warning (no retry).
- **Validation (2026-10-08 12:39 IST):** `npx jest test/unit/wm` -> **54 suites, 1093/1093 PASS**; `npx cds compile srv` -> PASS; `cd app/fiori-app && npx ui5lint` -> no findings; `git diff --check` -> clean.

### 2026-10-08 14:26 IST — Pre-Part-B: attempt-store expiry for 261/262 'unconfirmed', clearing a stuck one, and deferrals

- **Requested by user before Part B.** Read-only analysis (no code).
- **Expiry of an 'unconfirmed' 261 attempt:** the only background re-check timer is the **201** `goods-issue/service.js` one (interval 1 min default via GI_ATTEMPT_RECHECK_INTERVAL_MS; **skipped under NODE_ENV=test**; requires GoodsIssueService to be running in the deployed CAP process). `recheck` filters by Status only (not MovementType), so it DOES pick up 261/262 rows. For a **261** attempt the stored `ReferenceDocument` is the same `GI<hash>` our post sets as the material-document header ReferenceDocument, so `GoodsIssuePostingClient.findPostedByReference` (filters `A_MaterialDocumentHeader` by ReferenceDocument, matches GoodsMovementType '261' client-side) can resolve it: at age >= recheckAgeMs (3 min) -> `posted` if SAP has the doc, else at >= notPostedAgeMs (15 min) -> `not_posted`.
- **262 reversal caveat:** a reverse's attempt key is `RV<hash>`, but SAP's `CancelItem` copies the **original 261's** ReferenceDocument onto the 262 (it takes no reference param). So the recheck lookup by `RV<hash>` will NOT find the 262 -> a timed-out-but-actually-posted reversal is mislabeled `not_posted` at ~15 min. QA / operators must verify a reverse timeout in MB51, not by the attempt status.
- **Clearing a stuck attempt:** there is **no app/admin mechanism** - the store does create + setStatus only (no DELETE.from / reset; the entity is not projected into any CDS service, so no OData DELETE; `clearMemoryStore` only wipes the in-memory Map, a test/fallback helper). Because `createOrGet` ignores Status, even a resolved row ('posted'/'not_posted') keeps blocking a re-claim under the same (reservation+open-qty) key, so a legitimate retry after a **confirmed non-post of the same quantity** is blocked (409) until the row is removed. Today that requires a **DBA to delete the GoodsIssuePostingAttempt row**; after a successful post the open qty changes so the key differs and no clearing is needed.
- **DEFERRED (logged, NOT built now, per user):**
  1. **261/262-aware recheck** wired to `Mvt261Adapter` (so a 261 post / 262 reverse timeout is SAP-confirmed by the right adapter, and the 262 reference mismatch is handled) - separate change.
  2. **A self-service "reset / cancel attempt" action** (or an admin UI) to clear a stuck `unconfirmed`/`not_posted` attempt without a DBA, since `createOrGet` blocks re-claim regardless of status.
- **No live SAP data was modified.**

### 2026-10-08 17:13 IST — Incoterms value help returned "No Data" — root cause + fix; "Find Sales Incoterms" discovery

- **Trigger:** user — "Select Incoterms: No Data coming in Incoterms list", then "Find Sales Incoterms".
- **Root cause (why no data):** SO value-help entity sets are served by **explicit custom READ handlers** registered from `srv/sd/sales-order/handlers/valueHelp.config.js` via `registerValueHelpHandlers` (`srv/handlers/valueHelp.handler.js`). Only entities listed in `soValueHelpConfig` get a handler + backend reader; everything else returns empty. `IncotermsClassificationVH` (added as a CDS projection earlier today) was **not** in the config, so its OData read had no reader → empty list. (Same reason it worked for Payment Terms/Plant, which are in the config.)
- **Fix (minimal, reuse the proven maint path):** `srv/sd/sales-order/handlers/valueHelp.config.js` — added `IncotermsClassificationVH` to the `maint`/`readMaintData` group (the same group as `PlantVH`; `read: (query) => purchaseOrderAdapter.readMaintData(query)` → `MM_PUR_PO_MAINT_V2_SRV`), with `entityDeduplicateBy.IncotermsClassificationVH = 'IncotermsClassification'`, and added it to `SO_VALUE_HELP_ENTITIES`. This is the identical mechanism the Purchase Order service uses to serve its own `IncotermsClassificationVH` (its `MAINT_VALUE_HELP_ENTITIES` → `readMaintData`), proven live.
- **"Find Sales Incoterms" — discovery (per AGENTS.md, from the live catalog scans; GET-only, no SAP writes):** searched `incoterms-capability-scan.csv` + the already-imported SD metadata (`SD_F1873_SO_WL_SRV`, `SD_F2370_INQY_WL_SRV`, `SD_F2369_INQY_FS_SRV`). **There is no SD/Sales-specific Incoterms value-help entity set.** SD services expose `IncotermsClassification` only as a *document property* (analytics queries `C_SALESVOLUMEANALYTICSQRY_CDS`, `C_SLSORDCONFANLYTSQRY_CDS`, …), never as a lookup list. The only standalone Incoterms VH lists in the catalog are the module-agnostic/MM ones: `C_MM_IncotermValueHelp(Type)` (MM_PUR_PO_MAINT_V2_SRV, C_PURCHASEORDER_FS_SRV, and other MM maintain services) and the standard `I_IncotermsClassificationType`. Incoterms classification is **client-wide config (TINC)**, not module-scoped, so the MM VH returns exactly the incoterms valid for Sales. Conclusion: the already-wired `MM_PUR_PO_MAINT_V2_SRV` → `C_MM_IncotermValueHelp` is the correct and only real Incoterms VH; there is no separate Sales source to switch to.
- **Validation (2026-10-08 17:13 IST):**
  - `npx cds compile srv` → OK.
  - `npx jest test/unit/sales-order test/unit/sales-inquiry test/unit/purchase-order` → **40 suites, 664/664 PASS** (covers the shared value-help handler + the maint reader used by PlantVH/Incoterms).
  - `git diff --check` → clean. This fix: 1 file (`valueHelp.config.js`, +4/−2).
- **Not verified live (blocked):** a running-server read of `/odata/v4/sales-order/IncotermsClassificationVH`. The dev-server launch config references another session's local dev-password file; reading it was denied (credential-exploration guard) and I did not work around it, so I could not start the backend to prove the list now populates. The fix reuses the exact reader + external entity that the PO feature uses live for the same VH, so high confidence; a quick check on the user's running server (restart to pick up the handler, then open the Incoterms F4) will confirm.
- **No live SAP data was modified.**
- **Result:** PASS (code) — handler registered so `IncotermsClassificationVH` now has a backend reader; "no data" cause removed. Live confirmation pending a server restart on the user's running instance. Persistence of the chosen Incoterms is still Blocked on Issue 11 (unchanged).

### 2026-10-08 17:17 IST — Sales Orders listing (`#/sd/sales-orders`): action button overflowed its column — widened Action column

- **Trigger:** user — "Listing UI not proper, some button outside boundary."
- **Diagnosis:** In `app/fiori-app/webapp/modules/sd/sales-order/view/SalesOrders.view.xml` the per-row **Action** column was `width="8rem"` but holds a Transparent button "Create Delivery" (i18n `btnCreateDelivery`, 15 chars) with a leading `shipping-status` icon — ~10–11rem of content. The button overflowed the 8rem cell past the table's right edge. (The header `OverflowToolbar` self-manages overflow, so the only per-row button is the culprit.)
- **Fix (minimal):** Action column `width="8rem"` → **`width="12rem"`** so the labelled button fits inside the cell. No other change; button label/behaviour preserved. (`demandPopin="true" minScreenWidth="Tablet"` unchanged, so smaller screens still pop the column in.)

Fix is a deterministic column-width change for the described overflow; asked the user to confirm on their running instance (or name the specific button if a different one).

### 2026-10-08 17:31 IST — Create Sales Inquiry (`#/sd/sales-inquiries/create`): fixed 4 debug findings (#1–#4)

- **Trigger:** user "Debug what are the issues" then "Fix All" for the create-inquiry flow. Four issues diagnosed and fixed.
- **#1 Duplicate inquiry on re-submit after a partial create (High) — FIXED.** `app/fiori-app/webapp/modules/sd/sales-inquiry/controller/CreateSalesInquiry.controller.js` `onSave` catch: on a `PartialSalesInquiryError` (header created, item/price POST failed) the warning dialog's `onClose` now calls `that._resetModel(true)` for every action, clearing the form so the identical payload cannot be posted again and create a duplicate header in SAP. Mirrors the Sales Order verification-failed reset pattern.
- **#2 `getCustomerDefaults` had no error handling (Medium) — FIXED.** `srv/sd/sales-inquiry/handlers/salesInquiry.handler.js`: wrapped the adapter call in try/catch → `LOG.error` + `req.error(error.status || 502, message)` (was delegating raw, surfacing a 500). Now consistent with the Sales Order handler.
- **#3 `getInquiryCreationCapabilities` failed unguarded both ends (Medium) — FIXED.** Backend handler now `async (req)` with try/catch → `req.error(error.status || 502, message)`. Frontend `_loadCapabilities` now has a `.catch` that logs and resolves to the current/empty capabilities (no unhandled rejection); capabilities keep their safe default (`NO_CAPABILITIES`, fields stay disabled) on a transient SAP/RFC failure instead of throwing.
- **#4 Contact Person was a plain input with no value help (Low/UX) — FIXED.** Added a customer-filtered Contact Person value help, mirroring the Sales Order one (reuses the proven shared `salesInquiryAdapter.getContactPersons` reader + the shared `ValueHelpService` `/ContactPersonVH` config):
  - `srv/sd/sales-inquiry/service.cds` — new `ContactPersonVH` entity (ContactPerson/Customer/FirstName/LastName/FullName/PhoneNumber), same shape as the SO one.
  - `srv/sd/sales-inquiry/handlers/valueHelp.config.js` — `ContactPersonVH` added to `SD_VALUE_HELP_ENTITIES` and a new group `{ read: getContactPersons, filterInMemory: true, dedupe ContactPerson }`.
  - `CreateSalesInquiry.view.xml` — Contact Person input gains `showValueHelp`/`showSuggestion`/`suggest`/`valueHelpRequest`/`suggestionItemSelected=".onContactPersonSelect"` + `suggestionItems` bound to `salesInquiry>/ContactPersonVH` (key ContactPerson / additionalText FullName). Capability gating (`enabled`/`required`) kept.
  - `CreateSalesInquiry.controller.js` — added `onContactPersonSelect`; added a `Customer eq SoldToParty` context filter for `inContactPerson` in both `onSuggest` and `onValueHelpRequest`; added the `inContactPerson` write-back branch in the value-help dialog callback.
- **Not changed (verified fine during debug):** value-help entities all resolve; mapper does not inject assumed Ship-To/PO date; Net Price/Amount columns present; partial-failure classification correct.
- **Tests added (+6):** `createSalesInquiryHandler.test.js` — getCustomerDefaults (delegates, 502 on failure, 502 default when no status) and getInquiryCreationCapabilities (delegates, 502 on failure); `salesInquiryValueHelp.test.js` — ContactPersonVH is in `SD_VALUE_HELP_ENTITIES` and configured with getContactPersons + `filterInMemory` + dedupe.
- **Validation (2026-10-08 17:31 IST):** `npx cds compile srv` → OK; `npx jest test/unit/sales-inquiry test/unit/sales-order test/unit/purchase-order` → **40 suites, 670/670 PASS** (was 664 + 6 new); `cd app/fiori-app && npx ui5lint` → no findings; `git diff --check` → clean.
- **Not verified live:** running-app repro (dev server credential-gated this session). Fixes reuse proven SO patterns/readers and are unit-tested; a server restart + create-inquiry run confirms end to end. Contact Person VH data loads only when SAP/RFC is reachable (KNVK read, same as SO).
- **No live SAP data was modified.**
- **Result:** PASS — #1 duplicate-on-retry closed, #2/#3 error handling added, #4 Contact Person value help added; tests + lint + compile green.
